There is a charge for forgetting your password.**" That is the fee-sheet argument of [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]], made to a customer. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Mega_Hit_Demo_and_Trade_In-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]
- **Onboarding a location:** "**They spend one day filling in their information, and it takes twenty-four hours for approval. Then we are ready to go live**… Everything happens on the portal." The cloud dashboard also answers an inspection requirement the customer raised: "**a driver may come to the location and ask them for a report of [cards] issued. This can be done.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]

### Val's own recommended configuration — given twice, against his own product interest

Asked in both meetings what he would buy if he were the operator, Val gave the same answer and twice told them to **wait on the ATM**: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Narayani_Amusements_Mega_Hit_Demo_and_Trade_In-September-1-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]

> "**I suggest you wait until GLC and the GLC lawyer rule on the regulation for cash dispensing. So I suggest you don't do it. You wait**… not to do the IQ, but just to do the **self kiosk with printer and bill acceptor, and dispensing the cartridge**. So bill acceptor, printer, and the cartridge dispenser in the kiosk… **And I would leave validators inside of the machines the way they are**, and add this and add that. That would be my setup. But of course, the operators [decide]."

And to Air Gaming: "**if I was an operator, I would prefer a standalone kiosk that takes your money, issues e wallet, and then exchanges the e wallet with the card**… because **this is the piece of equipment that can grow with you, and this does not require an attendant, and this stands next to the machine.**" He bracketed it both times — "**If you ask me which I prefer, I can tell you which I prefer. But it's not up to me.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]

The Lego framing was repeated verbatim to the second customer: "**Think about it as a Lego set, and you and your location and your players. You choose the Lego pieces that you want in your sandbox.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]

### The anti-duplication architecture — why the cloud has to be the source of truth

The Air Gaming visitor raised ticket-copying fraud on their Texas machines, and the answer is the clearest statement in the corpus of **why** the system is cloud-based. See [[wiki/organizations/air-gaming-group.md]] and [[wiki/projects/texas-used-machine-distribution.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]

- **Why copying works against local validation.** "**the kiosk doesn't know which number is valid and which one is not. So if the kiosk thinks that this number is valid, then I reprinted this value. It also seems valid.**" And why a competitor's kiosk still rejects a foreign ticket without being any safer: "**that's not connected to anything that is keeping track of what happens. [Primero] only validates their own.**" Andrei sharpened it: "as soon as you're using a specific model of printer, this kiosk **may verify if the number looks correct or not, because they know the serial numbers**… **But it doesn't verify if this ticket is unique, if it's valid, and if it was already used or not.**"
- **The JVL flow, stated in full by Val.** "**your printer says, 'I have a request to issue a ticket. What ticket number should I issue?' That request goes to the cloud. The cloud says, 'Issue serial number one two three four for the value of twenty dollars.'** … When you bring this for validation, you present it to the reader, to optical reader. **Optical reader says to the cloud server, 'I'm reading ticket number one two three four for the value of twenty dollars. Is this legitimate?' And that number is always encrypted. So then the cloud server says, 'Legitimate. Pay it.'**" And: "**it has to be encrypted both ways.**"
- **The principle, from Andrei, generalised beyond JVL.** "**Not local. First of all, which is not related to any piece of your hardware. The source of truth should exist somewhere and be controlled not by the location. When the transaction or e-wallet is created, this source of truth is the only place where verification may happen. Then, when something is presented, it is being verified that it exists. This is the only way we protect.**"
- **The consequence stated as a guarantee.** "**If two identical tickets are presented, only one of them will be valid**"; "**With this system, you will have no fraud**"; "**I can actually give you a guarantee that people cannot copy tickets**… **I guarantee to you that they will not copy. Guaranteed**… **I can sign my name on** [it]." He offered the demonstration to match: "**Put it in the back room. Let people put a photocopier. Give them these things and put a photocopier next to it.**"
- **Updates and warranty**, asked in the same breath: updates are "**automatic**"; warranty is "**standard warranties… I think it's ninety days, but we can extend warranties.**"

### The commercial argument for Flex ports, restated

"**It's just a smart way of generating a lot of play. If you make your player move from machine to machine, eventually people get enough entertainment and they don't cash in. But if your environment is like today, you have to cash in and go back and you get a card, then chances are you're going to keep your winnings. That's not what you want.**" And Val's honest answer to "give me the cheapest thing just to comply": "**If you say, 'Give me the cheapest thing for Georgia,' just to comply with the law, you don't need any of these units**" — followed immediately by the reason to buy them anyway. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Air_Gaming_Group_Transactional_Walkthrough-September-1-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — device prices, monthly connection fees, install specifications and market economics only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 2 2026 — the pre-pilot punch list, the carrier-medium problem, and Val's target topology

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-flex-iq-pre-pilot-updates-after-the-show.md|Sept-2 pre-pilot call]] (Russian, 8:03–9:17 AM ET; [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]], [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dmitri Chudinov]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]]) the team fixed what Flex-IQ still needs before the first pilot location, then opened how IQ should work with Flex-Ports in a closed-loop wallet system. Rulings: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-activate-incomm-cards-by-barcode-on-flex-iq.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-implicit-consent-and-mandatory-printed-receipt.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-e-wallets-move-to-the-cloud.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-flex-port-sas-universal-with-scanner-touch-vs-non-touch.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-priorities-iq-ports-flex-pay-recycler-texas-on-wallets.md]].

- **Val's framing, deliberately parked rather than debated.** The first installation runs on top of Mutha Goose, but "сегодня уже всем понятно, что мы работать с мут[а гус] не будем и не можем. И вообще это даже неправильный был подход" — everyone now understands JVL will not and cannot work with Mutha Goose, and the approach was wrong in the first place; Flex-Port "должен работать сам по себе со своим [wiring], со своими проводами. То есть это значит, что мы должны вести провода к машинам." He explicitly refused to make it the subject of the call — "я не хотел бы сейчас… делать это целью этого разговора… Просто чтобы вы держали это в курсе" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **Andrei's reply reduced the risk of the parked question:** "те доработки, которые мы сейчас будем обсуждать, они процентов на 80 пригодятся и для… работающего с портами, поэтому вполне универсальные доработки" — roughly 80% of the changes carry over to a port-based setup [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - Val drew two consequences: some of the changes "требуют более дешевого флекспорта," and "флекспорт, скорее всего, не должен поддерживать машину, в которой есть только один с[ас] порт" — Flex-Port probably should not support a machine with only one SAS port [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **The carrier-medium problem, stated plainly.** Andrei: Flex-Ports "дают… игрокам возможность перемещаться между машинами. Это офигенное требование… рынок хочет, игроки любят," but "возникает вопрос, как нам инициировать создание [и]волета, потому что нашему [и]волюту нужен carr[i]er какойто носитель" — the e-wallet needs a physical carrier [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]. Four options were worked:
  1. **IQ with its printer at the cashier is the only place a carrier can be created.** With ports and bill validators on the machines and nothing else, "единственная точка, где физически [carrier] может быть создан. Это IQ со своим принтером" — the player must approach the cashier for a wallet or "просто сказать, дай мне пустой ticket." Andrei's own verdict: "Работает[,] странный опыт, возможно, но… окей." Redemption is straightforward — the player returns, the cashier scans the carrier on IQ, "IQ показывает сумму," and a packaged card is activated [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  2. **Hybrid: a slim Flex printer kiosk on the floor.** "на локации стоит… [флекс] принтер… это такой же киоск как наши[,] Возможно, в слим[-]факторе, и всё, что он умеет делать, это печатать пустой [и]волет [carrier]," with IQ reduced to redemption and card issue. "флоу более автономный[:] игрок заходит на локацию, он не идёт к кассиру, он идёт сразу в гейм [room]… Со временем у него развивается привычка, что чтобы начать играть[,] Я сначала печатаю [carrier]" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  3. **Pavel's stack of pre-printed tickets at the location — rejected by Andrei on three grounds:** "Их теряют"; "их заливают водой, сминают, выкидывают… создаёт очень грязное ощущение"; and "наверное, даже самое главное[:] А кто будет ответственным за печать этих билетов[,] за их пополнение? Вот кто[,] локация[?] мы не можем на это рассчитывать. А если тикетов не будет на локации, проблема напрямую адресуется нам" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  4. **Pavel's QR code passed to the player's phone.** Andrei liked it in principle — "как только появляется смартфон в этом уравнении[,] это открывает огромные возможности… нам нужно… какуюто страницу создаём [JVL-]скую, где у пользователя появляется. Свой кабинет, где он может пополнять там кредиты" — but flagged a hypothesis against it: "игроки. Конкретно в штате Джорджия не очень расположены к использованию своего смартфона для игр по разным причинам" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Val's summing-up on the carrier question — agreement without knowledge.** "Я хочу, чтоб мы все остались под впечатлением, что… Мы все согласны что… Смартфон это хорошее решение. Но… никто из нас сегодня не знает, это будет [accept]ед игроком или нет[.] Но нам нужно нача[ть] идти кудато." What the team can grasp with its own hands today, "Правильно это [или] неправильно[,] Это печатание пел[л]етов." Where it goes after that "Пока непонятно." He mentioned giving each Flex-Port "Свой излучаемый идентифика[тор]" as a possible future direction but explicitly "не сегодняшнего дня решения" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **The business constraints Val then set, refusing to propose engineering** ("Я бы не хотел бы предлагать инженерные решения… Я бы хотел бы только обсудить [use] [ca]s[es]. И некоторые… [pillars] o[f] [the] бизнес[а]") [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]:
  - **Unit cost must be minimised, because JVL will likely give the hardware away or subsidise it.** "нам все это нужно будет какимто образом отдавать бесплатно или покрывать какимто… [rebate]… Возмещением какимто… Поэтому себестоимость должна быть минимальная на всё. Потому что все это, скорее всего, будет выходить из нашего кармана," whether "мы это отдадим оператору или оператор это отдаст лакейшину." This restates the model on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-free-hardware-fixed-monthly-fee-model.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **He endorsed Andrei's slim printer-plus-card-dispenser complex:** "то что предлагает Андрей… Комплекс[,] слим[,] поставить принтер и кард[-]диспенсер. Я согласен с этим, это хорошее решение" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **A hard requirement: remote access to disconnect a non-paying location.** "мы должны иметь [access] на локешене. Потому что если… лакейшин перестал платить деньги, нам нужно иметь возможность… Отсоединить это вот системы. Потом заморозит[ь] их… работу." Val labelled it himself: "Вот это жесткий как бы [requirement]" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Val's target topology — the host at the centre, a flexible mix of local devices.** "гвоздь программы нашей это х[о]ст. И это куча каких[-]то разных лок[ал] девайс[ов][.] Которые могут подключаться какимто образом… через… Сеть какуюто параллельную." The device list he named [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]:
  - today's wide Flex-Port — "какието порты с расширенными [capabilities], как сейчас у нас есть";
  - a narrowed budget port — "порты суженными… бюджетн[ые]";
  - "просто сми бы, которые внутри стоят там за **$9**. На машине";
  - "флекс IQ. Или на больших лакейш[е]нах может быть пару";
  - "какието флекс пей… В рамках слим[-]продукта[,] в котор[ом] стоит и валидатор и карт[-][ди]спенсер. И ридер. И экран… [для] [интер]акшен с игроком";
  - plus "какойто репортинг[?] Dash[board]" and "[accessibility] каких[-]то мобильных девайсов".
  - His requirement on the mix: "топография… она должна быть гибкая… [Девайсы], которые будут прикреплены… не жёсткие, они довольно флекс[ибл]. И номенклатура этих девайсов тоже должна быть. Широкая" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **Where he expects it to go:** "В свое время наверно IQ перерастёт в какойто мобильный девайс, то есть можно сделать полностью replac[e]ment этого [устройства]"; and if players adopt digital wallets, "можно уйти от какихто экранов. Прой[ти] к какимто [SMIB-ам][,] Которые сидят… может быть на са[с]-порту[,] И, может быть, машина сама будет [коммуницировать] потом через какойто… API." He labelled it as speculation: "я немножко помечтал здесь" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Multiple card providers restated as a business pillar.** Andrei: "нам надо ещё… Поддержка… разных… карт[-]провайдер[ов], то есть сейчас у нас только инк[омм], но нам надо уметь поддерживать всех, кто есть на рынке." Val: "Абсолютно. Точно." This re-affirms [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-27-flex-iq-multiple-clearinghouses-selectable-per-device.md]] and widens it from clearing houses to card providers generally [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Remote software update — state of the work.** Dmitri Chudinov reported it "оказалась чуть более… Сложнее, чем… с Андреем ожидали, так как мы приняли решение работать чере[з] Amazon," which pulled in terminal-side work and "работ[у] там с [Kubernetes]… по всем слоям." Where it stands: he is running final passes with positive cases. CI builds a new version, "она паблишится на Amazon… специальном хранилище, у которого… Быстрая… скачивание[;] к нему не будет доступа… и Андрей[у] не надо будет на [GitHub] лазить" — nobody needs GitHub access. The operator gets "доступ в нашу систему транзакционного, где будет отдельная… роль… оператор, который производит… Развертывание… терминалов," with a dashboard "вот наши терминалы, что на нём установлено," picks terminals and the latest or a chosen bundle, and deploys; "Это всё будет в онлайне скачиваться на терминалы," after which the operator can force a reboot "либо сделать это в какоето время, но зачастую ночью" and "терминал скажешь, что он обновлён" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **The UI is JVL's own, on the transactional dashboard behind a separate role.** Pavel: "[Бэкенд] это использует… готовые там [API] сторонние. График[еский] [user] интерфейс это там [Дима] делал сам." Andrei confirmed his reading: "это будет лежать в нашем [transactional] дэшборд[е,] по отдельно[й] роль[ю] доступ к этому" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **Built deliberately to extend to future terminals.** Chudinov: "я стараюсь на будущее… чтоб это было расширяемо[,] все следующие терминалы типа flex pay, carler и так далее" — the second name is a garble the derived note reads as the recycler [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]. Pavel added that the other kiosks inherit it free if they run **RK3568**: "если мы используем для остальных киосков р к 35 68, то… все вот эти вот обновления они автоматически уже будут поддерживаться" — bar the registration step, "в остальном… они по сути одинаковые." This is the same platform argument that carried [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-01-flex-pay-onto-rk3568.md]] the day before [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **Rollback will be tested.** Andrei raised "возможность отката к последней рабочей версии"; Chudinov: "я буду всё на это прогонять тесты с возможностью отката." Andrei's gate before a live location: "перед запуском живой локаци[и]… это надо тестировать… Поставили несколько юнитов, подключили их в сеть[,] накат[или] обновления." Timing: "на следующей неделе, возможно, мы уже начнём у себя тестировать" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
  - **The new boards do not affect it.** Andrei asked directly; Chudinov and Pavel both answered "Нет," Pavel adding "это просто новой прошивкой обновится[,] поддержка[,] добавится этот [борд]" — only new firmware adding support for the board [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Payment hub — everything Nathan built is deployed.** Pavel: "там всё что было сделано [Нейтоном] оно за[]деплоино" (see [[wiki/people/nathan-carver.md]]). The agreed test: Andrei puts enough money on the card and Pavel re-runs the API request right after the meeting "чтобы транзакции, которые подвисли, которые были ошибочные, чтобы они перевелись в пендинг, и они, по идее, утром завтра попытаются повторно сделать [ACH]." Andrei: "я тогда закину денег… и посмотрим завтра утром… Считайте, что договорились." On the retry hour, Pavel thought it is tied to wherever Dwolla sits — "привязано к какойто локации, где там [Dwolla] живёт, в каком регионе… В 08:00 их времени" — Chudinov guessed "джорджевская… таймзона," Pavel "либо Джорджия, либо там… Центральноамериканская." **The zone is unresolved; only the 08:00 hour was agreed** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Irina Shishko forced the schedule question.** When Pavel moved to close the punch-list and go to strategy, she stopped him: "нет, подожди Паш… Я про то, что мы не определили план, когда мы… Реализуем первую часть." Andrei backed her — "я бы хотел тоже это проговорить ещё раз зафиксировать" — which produced Chudinov's end-of-week commitment and Andrei's Tuesday test [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]].
- **§15.8:** clear — device, board, memory and market economics only; no named-person compensation. **§15 #9:** clear — no corporate digital identifiers appear in either source.

## Sept 2 2026 (lunch) — the recycler explained mechanically to InComm, and the IQ-replaces-PAX offer

Hours after the internal pre-pilot call above, Val described the **recycler** to [[wiki/organizations/incom.md|InComm]] in close mechanical detail *(a similar cassette-by-cassette account had been given to a customer, Epic, on Aug 25 — see above)* ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-incomm-machine-payments-and-recycler-standoff.md|the lunch]]). The commercial outcome — InComm refusing it — is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-hold-the-recycler-back-but-sign-no-undertaking.md]]; what belongs here is how the device works and what he offered alongside it [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].

### The economic premise: the refill cycle, not the cash

- "**The most expensive part of any ATM is [the] refill cycle.**" The pitch, set deliberately in a variety store in Oklahoma rather than in Georgia: "I will **recycle this money**. I will **sort this money into recycling cassettes**… And whenever I have to give change, I'll give change from those recycling cassettes. And better yet — **I'm going to act as an ATM**… instead of a bank coming in through **Loomis** or whatever security company that delivers the bills, **you guys will pocket this money as well as the ATM charges**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Capacity is not the constraint; replenishment is.** "it's not really how much cash; it's **how it's refilled**. So if you're constantly withdrawing, withdrawing, withdrawing, you're gonna drain it. But if you keep on refilling, sorting, and you could manage cash smartly, then you're fine. **Capacity does matter. But with enough sorting cassettes, you could manage it**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].

### Two cassette systems, and why worn bills work

- **The device has two halves.** Asked what happens at a location taking in $6,000 a day and loading $4,000 onto cards, Val corrected the framing: "**I know where you're making the mistake. The device consists of two parts: the recycling holding cassettes and the drop cassette. The drop cassette is where [the] majority of the money is.**" The recycling cassettes are never refilled from outside — "**you never collect it, sort it, refill it**" — which is where the cost of cash goes out [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **The mechanism is a two-tape reel, not a stack — and that is what makes worn notes usable.** "The quality of the bill for [an] ATM versus [a] recycler is totally different because an **ATM stockpiles the bills**… have you seen old **magnetic tape recorders from the '70s with two reels**? … the same type of tape is used in the **recycling cassettes**. But two of them, one inner, one out[er], and **the bills go between the two tapes**. So there is a roller that rolls it, and instead of stacking it like this, **it rolls it between the two tapes. So [it] winds it in, and when change is given, it unwinds it. So there is never a stacking issue. So I could use worn-out bills. It's a huge cost component**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Sorting also solves shrinkage** — "aside from [the] security and integrity part of it… **the small operator always has shrinkages**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].

### The carrier medium — the same list, to an outside party, the same day

Asked what a player physically walks away with, Val gave the identical option set the team had spent the morning arguing about (above): "we could issue an electronic, temporary electronic wallet in the form of… **anything that carries a bar code or QR code**, or sends a QR code or a bar code **to your phone**, or using a **cigarette box code paired with a PIN** code. It doesn't really matter what you pair it to, **as long as you have a carrying medium that you can present to another reader**… **it could be your card, it could be a printed ticket, it could be a QR code**." And the whole function of the device in one line: "**I'm taking money, I'm sorting money, and I'm keeping the money until the payout is made. And that's all I'm doing**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].

He also gave the product's own history: in the 1990s and 2000s the CashCode ambition was for these devices "**to replace TITO by being in a machine, sitting inside of a slot machine**" — asked why it never happened, "**Regulations? No. Reliability, size, cost**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].

### The Flex-Port, described to an outsider: validator emulation, SAS 2, and promo credits

- **Where a machine has only one SAS port, the port impersonates the validator.** "our ports can… **mimic a validator**… here's a validator. It's plugged into a motherboard. We remove that plug. We put our port. We plug the validator into our port, and our port into [the] validator port. **So we emulate a validator**… pretend that we are counting pulses" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Where there are two, the port sits on SAS 2 and behaves like casino player tracking.** "**majority of the market today, 90% of the market has two SAS ports**… then our device behaves like a **player tracking system in a class three casino**, which has [an] enormous amount of capabilities" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **The capability he volunteered, and flagged as regulator-bait himself: promo credits.** "we can easily **on SAS two port issue promo credit**. So we can send the promo credit to your phone number and say, go in this location **between 3 p.m. and 4 p.m.** And here's a validation QR code. Scan it at our port, and here's **$20**." His own caveat: "**Lottery is loud and clear that we're not allowed to give any incentives. This [is] not an incentive. This [is] a free play, and it registers on a promo credit meter, which does not go into a counter**" — prefaced with "maybe this is gonna freak out lottery corporation again." **Recorded as a claimed capability and Val's own reading of the rules, not as a compliance finding** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].
- **And the churn argument the ports exist to serve, stated to a payments company for the first time:** with MudaGoose, a player who thinks a machine has gone cold "presses [the] collect button. The collect button **makes him get off his chair**," walk to the counter and cash out. With ports and a temporary wallet, "they traverse through play. They experience **all of the machines in the location**" and cash out less, "**because eventually, the longer time you spend in a location, the more chances that you will spend**… It's called **churns**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].

### The IQ point-of-sale offered as a replacement for PAX

Val's commercial proposal — sell InComm the JVL POS at the PAX price, remove MudaGoose, rewire the location, and give the ports away on the back of a machine sale — is recorded with InComm's bounty counter-offer on [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]] (Sept 2), and its enforcement consequence on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]]. The device is named here as "**the IQ point of sale**," and the customer's-eye version of it — tabletop, seen at the show — got the reaction "**Yeah, tabletop. Very very sexy device**… I think it has… It solves a lot of problems" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]].

## Sept 2 2026 (Lucky Coin) — the catalogue sold to an operator, with the ATM half deliberately left out

The same catalogue was put in front of a customer that morning ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-lucky-coin-service-and-upgrade-offer.md]]), and what is notable is what was **withheld**: the ATM side was removed from the offer before the customer could ask for it. Pricing is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].

- **The slim unit, as offered:** "this form fit, **slim form fit, with a scanner, with a bill acceptor, and a printer**… **Without the ATM side of it**" — corrected moments later to the full list, "**card dispenser, printer, bill acceptor, and scanner**." Val's reason for the omission, said out loud to the customer: "the ATM side is, you know, **rubs the [GLC] the wrong way, and we need to figure that one out**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **The "Lego set" framing again, credited to Andrei.** Val: "when I'm pointing to this, I'm pointing to **form fit factor**. What this could house inside, I'm gonna use **[Andrei]'s expression**, is a **Lego set**. And you could assemble or disassemble whatever you want" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **The carrier medium, described to a customer:** "This becomes your **electronic wallet**… This is a **carrying medium**. It could be a **cigarette pack**. It could be **plastic cards**." And the argument for spending money on a proper card rather than loose ones: "we recommend **not to be stingy and create comfort**… it's also a little bit of reassurance, **evidence of a play, evidence of money spent**… **Loose cards, no matter how you organize them, [are] not good hygiene in the location**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **First buy-in still needs a human or a kiosk.** [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei]]: "**If you don't have this in [the] place, they have to start with a cashier. They need to get an empty ticket or hand cash to your cashier in exchange for a ticket. But after that, they can add additional cash right there**" — the same cashier-or-kiosk dependency the morning's carrier-medium debate turned on [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Multiple card providers, restated to a customer.** Andrei: "**Right now, it's [InComm]. When [another provider] comes in, you will have both**… It's a choice." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-27-flex-iq-multiple-clearinghouses-selectable-per-device.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].
- **Ports mount on any machine on the route:** "this one is **mounted to every game machine you can find in your collection**… It plugs into [SAS] two ports," needing only brackets [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].

## Sept 2 2026 (Continental) — the competitor price sheet JVL has to beat, and Val quoting retail on purpose

The same afternoon ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-continental-bifurcated-operations-and-vendor-lock-in.md|~3:32 PM ET]]) a Georgia operator running **Access on ~75% of his estate** laid out what his two incumbents charge him and what JVL would have to displace. This is the most complete competitor price comparison in the corpus, and it is competitive intelligence rather than a JVL design decision. [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

**Access — what the incumbent costs.** "**Charges depend on the machines. About fifteen dollars a machine. Plus the whole system is about three to four thousand dollars**" — and separately, "**I paid five thousand dollars for a nine machine install.**" Val's read of that last figure: it is a **wholesale** price (see below). *(The $15/machine figure sits close to the ~$15/machine/month the corpus already carries for "Access" from Felipe's Jul-7 2026 report, above — but this source gives no period, so the two are recorded side by side and not merged.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Bifurcated_Operations_Texas_and_Virginia-September-2-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

**Novomatic — the model Val has to beat, and the part that matters is who pays.** Until now the corpus has carried Novomatic only as a Class-III machine maker and a German AWP incumbent; here it appears as a **transactional/card competitor in Georgia**, being pushed into every location Access does not already hold. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

- **The price.** "**It's two hundred and ten dollars per month, and we don't have to buy anything but the printers**… **two hundred and ten dollars a month for thirty-six months, and then it goes to one hundred and fifty dollars a month.**"
- **The printers are the only capex, and they are cheap.** "I have to buy printers, but I like them. **That's two hundred dollars in printers, right? Six machines, twelve hundred dollars.**"
- **The structural advantage: it is billed to the location, not the operator.** "**What I like about Novomatic is that two hundred and ten dollars doesn't come from my pocket. It comes directly from the sales point**… **Because they build** [bill] **directly to them.**" Val pushed on why a store would agree: "**Because they have this replay now, they don't have to worry about money to build** [bill] **that.**" The location accepts the bill because the replay function fixes its own Georgia Lottery reporting problem — the mechanism is on [[wiki/topics/georgia-prize-card-processor-economics.md]] (Sept 2, Continental).
- **So every non-Access location is going to Novomatic.** "**all my locations that they don't have access to, I push them to Novomatics.**" With Access as the stated fallback: "**if that goes bad, we'll just go back to access.**"

**JVL's own quote, given at retail on purpose.** Val priced the suite in the room: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

- "**our system is a thousand dollars per machine**… **A thousand dollars for the POS**," plus "**We charge ninety dollars per month**" — confirmed as **per location**, not per machine.
- **Wiring:** "**You would need to change wiring**… We could do either two SAS. **We recommend the second SAS, or you could plug them into Bill Accepted ports**… **But we strongly recommend SAS.**" This is the same second-SAS preference recorded throughout this page.
- **The positioning against Access:** "we could do **kiosks**. We could do a **self-checkout**. Things that **don't require an attendant**. **We also don't need printers. We're exactly like Access, just a little more expensive.**"
- **And the admission Val volunteered rather than negotiated:** "**Access gave you a wholesale price. I'm giving you a retail price. I'm giving you retail because you're not my customer.** But of course, when push comes to shove, and you would like to buy, we can sit down and talk."

**Val's own read of the competitor stack, said aloud:** "**Novomatic is a very good company. I love Novomatic.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

**Why this section matters to the product argument on this page.** The recurring-revenue premise recorded on [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-31-transactional-equipment-only-with-location-exclusivity.md]] assumes the operator is the payer. Novomatic's model moves the recurring fee **off the operator and onto the location**, funded by the location's own compliance benefit — which is a different commercial shape from JVL's $90/month-per-location charged to the operator, and no source records JVL considering it. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Continental_Vendor_Lock_In_Dead_End-September-2-2026.md]]

## Sept 2 2026 (evening) — the whole JVL flow demonstrated to an outsider, and the one step Val calls illegal

That evening ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-02-billy-liu-playpay-partnership-deep-dive.md|the PlayPay deep-dive]]) Val walked [[wiki/people/billy-liu.md|Billy Liu]] through the end-to-end player journey on the actual hardware. It is the most compact description of the target flow in the corpus, and the only place Val names its legal gap out loud to a third party. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]]

### The flow, as demonstrated

> "**So we remove bill acceptor from machine. No bill acceptor, no printer**… So a player comes in… **You get a ticket**… You walk now. **You walk through machines**… Do I need cash out? Play another machine. **So he cashes out. This is an electronic wallet. Presents the electronic wallet. Puts his pin number** — again, optional. **Goes to another machine. Scans it. Again, pin code. Again, puts in the money.**"

- **The barcode is optional at both ends** — "The bar, the code is optional. Don't worry about it" — said twice while handing over the carrier medium.
- **The player returns to the same terminal to redeem** — "Comes back to the same terminal" — and, after a colleague says "we have the fifteen", "**Dollars redeemable. Redeem… So redeeming. Confirm redemption.**"
- **And then the gap.** Val, reaching that screen: "**So that is illegal… Right now.** What is legal now, with your application, you send redeem now instead of Visa. Or put ten dollars on Visa and redeem fifteen dollars here. **This kiosk now redeems fifteen dollars.**" That is the whole commercial logic of the PlayPay conversation in three sentences — the wallet is the legal path through the step JVL's own device cannot take. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-val-proposes-fifty-percent-of-play-payments.md]] and, for the constraint that makes it so, [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-no-cash-from-coam-redemption-device-for-skill-pay.md]].

### The giveaway restated the same evening — $16,000, six units, Flex-IQ, over the second SAS

The morning's offer to a named operator ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]]) was restated to an outsider hours later, with a different machine price and the same bundle: "these are **expensive units. We sell them for a thousand bucks**… **This machine is sixteen thousand dollars. I'm going to give you one machine plus six of these units plus Flex IQ for free for sixteen thousand dollars.** You don't pay a penny more." The split is "**One unit goes into my machine. Plus, we have five more units.**" The derived notes read the bundle the same way — "a new machine at $16,000 including one unit inside it plus five more Flex ports plus Flex-IQ, connected over SAS 2" [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]]

- **Connection, stated the same way as everywhere else on this page:** "All of these units are installed and **connect to SAS protocol**… We could connect into a bill acceptor. We take the bill acceptor out, connect the bill acceptor here… **But in this case, this is not important because this will go to SAS. SAS two.**"
- **And a financing option raised nowhere else:** "**I may even rent these [units] or lease these [units].**" No terms were given. *("humans" is the transcript's rendering of "units" there.)*
- **The $16,000 sits against the $15,750 list price quoted that morning.** Both are recorded; neither source reconciles them, and the spread is consistent with the rounding Val uses with different audiences already noted on [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Lucky_Coin_Service_and_Upgrade_Offer-September-2-2026.md]].

### NFC on the ports — an outside patent offered, and Val's counter

Billy raised a patent he holds on NFC "**tap to fund**" and proposed marrying it to the Flex ports. Val's answer was that the port's scanner already covers it: "We can definitely put NFC… **But you know, I could read the QR code in here on your phone.**" Billy: "**Doesn't matter. NFC is what we made to load credits.**" **Nothing was decided and no work was assigned**; the open item sits on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-val-proposes-fifty-percent-of-play-payments.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]].

### The strategic argument for the whole catalogue, stated more plainly than anywhere else

Asked implicitly why he would give the hardware away, Val gave the reason the catalogue exists at all: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Billy_Liu_PlayPay_Partnership_Deep_Dive-September-2-2026.md]]

> "**you have the magic solution. But tomorrow, everybody will have this magic solution. There is no magic in this solution. The magic is in speed**… **You have to walk into location where location says, 'Do you have it?' I say yes. You have it? I say yes. How much does it cost? Nothing. Zero. That's how it needs to happen. Otherwise, it's not going to happen.**"

That is the same "answer yes to everything at zero cost" premise underneath the [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-free-hardware-fixed-monthly-fee-model.md|Aug-24 free-hardware model]] and the Sept-2 Lucky Coin offer — here given as a **time-limited** argument rather than a pricing one, which is new.

**§15.8:** clear — device, subscription and competitor pricing only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 4 2026 (midday) — the first ten Flex-Slims, the giveaway priced from the inside, and SAS moved onto Flex-Port

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-flex-slim-first-ten-and-flex-port-sas.md|Sept-4 midday production call]] (Russian, 12:29–1:49 PM ET; [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]], with [[wiki/people/evgeniy-alekseev.md|Evgeniy Alekseev]], [[wiki/people/eugine-yavorski.md|Eugine Yavorsky]], [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth Palomino]] and, late and from his car, [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]]) is the first source that turns the free-hardware pitch into a **parts list and a build schedule**. Rulings: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-brackets-and-harnesses-from-china-and-a-metal-order-for-one-hundred.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-flex-port-keeps-its-screen.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-port-the-smib-sas-module-into-the-flex-port-main-branch.md]].

### The first ten Flex-Slims, in parts

Val's restated target, spoken deliberately for the record: the first ten **Flex-Slim** units with **card dispenser, front-load bill acceptor, printer and the optical parts**, faster than the four weeks he had been quoted. Genadi's inventory: two Slims in hand and **eight more "где то в океане"** (supposed to have shipped mid-July); **twenty plastics already landed in Georgia** and not entered in the system ("Кроме меня сейчас, наверное, никто не знает"); **ten card dispensers bought, four installed in Flex-Pay units, six more in stock at about $380 each**; **twelve monitors**; the small interface board for printer and card dispenser with stock on hand; set wires already ordered; and the **RK3568** as the Slim's standard board. Full detail and the bracket critical path on the decision page [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].

A second procurement thread surfaced in passing: Genadi had spent time the previous day pushing Elizabeth Palomino and [[wiki/people/mikhail-perelman.md|Mikhail Perelman]] to get an **order of 100 Flex-Ports** moving — "чтобы она пошла, посчитала, чтобы он харнес[ы] начал заказывать" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].

### The giveaway costed out — the first itemised retail arithmetic in the corpus

Val told Genadi to take a pen and write the numbers down, then walked the bundle. **This is the same offer as [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-free-ports-and-pos-with-every-machine-bought.md]], but with a different composition and a different set of retail values** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]]:

| Item | Retail value stated Sept 4 | Quantity in the bundle |
|---|---|---|
| Flex-Port | "каждый ритейл прайс на порт **$795**" | 5 |
| Flex-IQ | "Один [IQ] это **$895** ритейл прайс" | 1 |
| Flex-Slim | "**$7950**" (Genadi's first guess: "Ну на 8000 будет") | 1 |
| **Total product given** | "**12820**" | |

Against that: "Оператор платит мне за машину **15[,]750**. Минус **12,820**… **$2930**. У оператора в голове цифра $2930, он покупает [машину]." The machine price was raised off the back of the show — "Мы раньше машины продавали по **13 500**" — and the competitive frame is "вместо, например, Аристократа за **20000** он покупает у нас машину за **16000**. И получает всё абсолютно всё забесплатно," plus "эс[а]бскр[и]п[шн] **$90 в месяц**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].

- **Composition differs from Sept 2.** Sept-2 was *six ports plus one terminal* (~$7,000 of hardware); Sept-4 is *one Flex-Slim + one Flex-IQ + five Flex-Ports* ($12,820). Both are recorded; **neither source mentions the other and no reconciliation is attempted here.**
- **The unit prices differ from the ones quoted to customers three days earlier.** On Sept 1 the port was "**nine hundred fifty dollars**" / "approximately a thousand" and the IQ "a thousand dollar device"; here they are **$795** and **$895**. The Sept-1 figures were explicitly framed as street retail for a single unit; the Sept-4 figures are given internally, to compute the value of a package. **Both stand; neither is corrected by the other.** See also [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]] (Sept 4, midday).
- **And the Flex-Slim at $7,950 is a third kiosk figure.** The slim kiosk without cash dispensing was quoted at "**approximately five thousand**" to customers on Sept 1 and Sept 2, and the kiosk with an ATM at "approximately twelve thousand." $7,950 sits between them and **nothing in the source says which configuration it is**. Recorded as spoken.
- **Val's stated aim for the offer, in his own words:** "По сути дела… мы в принципе должны **убить рынок**… этим оф[ф]ром. Рынок, я имею [в виду] тут о всех других" — kill the market, meaning everyone else. The market arithmetic behind it: "Джордж[ия] **47000 машин**… на **7400** лак[ей]ш[е]нов… у вас получится **5.9 машин на лакейшин**"; only "**20%** лакейш[е]нов имеют какого то сорта [систему]," and on the other 80 percent "им раздали забесплатно… так называемый **PAX**… [который] хреново работ[ает]." *(The 47,000 / 7,400 / 5.9 figures match the same-day statement recorded on [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]]; the 20/80 split of locations with and without a card system is new here.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]]
- **Why he is pushing tooling so hard:** "Вы понимаете, что я вас не просто так толкаю?… сделайте образцы, если образцы заработают[,] сразу переводите все на тулы." Genadi's reaction to the offer itself was simply "Ничего себе," and his first thought was the operator's: "Чтобы они могли ретрофит[нуть] весь лакейшен" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].

### SAS on Flex-Port, and Pavel's stage-2 debug environment

The SAS ruling is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-port-the-smib-sas-module-into-the-flex-port-main-branch.md]]. What belongs here is Pavel's counter-proposal, made from his car in the worst stretch of the recording. He accepted the port as "встреча один" (stage one, as transcribed) and named a second stage of his own [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]]:

- **Debug hooks and packet logging, or you debug blind.** "нам надо какието отладочные хуки для Того, чтобы удобно отслеживать… логирования какието пакеты данных, то есть на этапе тестирования это очень будет важно… чтобы не упустить какието… ошибки или там потери связи и там потери баланса… без этого… можно очень долго пытаться потом исправлять ошибки."
- **What he has already built for himself.** "Я сделал во первых там **эмулятор игровой машины**. Он… на 100%, конечно же, не повторяет машину, но большую часть [workflow] может обеспечить гораздо быстрее, чем это было бы с машиной," plus "**консоль отладки, полноценная с командами[,] с логами**." Asked what processor: "Это на **стм 32**. Там **743**."
- **He corrected Val on what Aaron built.** Val asked whether this was like the environment [[wiki/people/aaron.md|Aaron]] made for the RK3568. Pavel: "нет… **Аарон никакую среду не подымал**, он всего лишь собрал набор скриптов, которые позволяли собрать нужные модули[ ]и собрать корневую файловую систему для этого [RK] процессора." For STM, "поставщик… уже да[ю]т много всяких библиотек… среда[,] в общем-то[,] есть."
- **The environment is the surroundings, not the toolchain.** "нам для проведения тестов необходимо, например, **флекспей** подключен[ный]… нам нужна **машина, подключенная в сеть**[,] нам надо… подключенный **порт, возможно в нескольких экземплярах**… здесь у тебя есть, например, [о]тладчик… **Эстелинк**" — an **ST-Link** debugger — and then, crucially, a written description of what is connected where and where each unit's code lives, "и в принципе, он много чего находит и говорит, где что надо поправить" — "он" being Claude, which "может этот к[од] прям на лету менять[,] перезапускать."
- **Most of it exists and has been handed on.** "Большую часть я уже сделал… И показал это **[Хамину]** сегодня. Он может это взять, по крайней мере для своего тестирования" — see [[wiki/people/alexei-khamin.md|Alexei Khamin]], JVL's QA engineer.
- **How to write it up as capabilities — his rough shape, offered as a guess.** Chudinov's document "была схема работы не его [eWallet?], а… схема работы [тита]" *(the transcript renders a term repeatedly as "его лет"; by the pattern already recorded on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-flex-port-sas-universal-with-scanner-touch-vs-non-touch.md]] this is most likely an **eWallet** garble — flagged as inference, not asserted)*. His proposal: talk the scheme through out loud on Monday with the interested people, "и пусть Клод предложит, какие [capabilities]." His rough shape: "одна[ —] это обеспечить работу флекс порта в режиме **сас айфти транзакций** [SAS **AFT** transactions — "AFT" also appears in Latin script in the degraded English stretch of the same passage]… 2[ —] это использование на флекс порт[е]… [и] дополнительная вспомогательная [capability] о том, чтобы… **[debug] среду создать**… консоль или там эмулятор. Ну это можно такие тоже [capabilities] создать, либо это просто как фичи внутри [других]."
- **Val parked it to Monday:** "давайте вы подумайте на уикенд, и мы в понедельник соберёмся."

Two small items closed the call: Genadi asked for time on the recycler and Val deferred it — "У меня есть, но… мне надо на другой митинг идти сейчас" — and Val told Genadi what he made of the modem call that had ended minutes earlier (on [[wiki/people/pavel.md]]) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Slim_First_Ten_Build_and_FlexPort_SAS-September-4-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear — device, component and bundle pricing only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 7 2026 (8:00 AM) — the show stand becomes the product plan, and MudaGoose is condemned

The Monday's **first** meeting ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-gacs-show-setup-flex-iq-flex-ports-and-the-e-wallet.md|8:00–8:59 AM ET]], Russian, titled "FLEX-Port v-next discussion") is the only one of the three organised by [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]] rather than Val, and the only one that works from a written document — a Confluence page he had put in the chat. Nobody had read it, so Val imported a method: "Шеф Безос… заставляет всех читать и обсуждать вещи прямо на митинге. Считаю, что ни у кого не было возможности [про]читать[ся] Поэтому давайте мы пройдёмся… по [use-]к[ей]с[ам]" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

Rulings: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-flex-iq-cannibalises-mudagoose-no-parallel-operation.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-operating-mode-set-at-build-time-and-nothing-is-deleted.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-e-wallets-stay-on-the-local-gateway-until-jvl-has-its-own-cloud.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-the-e-wallet-must-exist-with-a-zero-balance.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-print-the-winnings-ticket-at-the-cashier-over-sas.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-one-tito-printer-and-one-paper-for-everything.md]].

**Val's framing, given before anything else: this is not a show exhibit.** "мы сейчас не просто говорим, что вот у нас будет трейд[-шоу]… Мы сейчас активно занимаемся продажей… определённых решений в Джорджии. Одн[о] из основных решений — это флекс[-]айкью и порты. И флекспей. Вот эта комбинация[.] Поэтому это не только для трейд[-шоу]… и для самого рынка." Klimovich agreed and returned the point sharpened: "шоу — это наш конкретный milestone с конкретной датой[,] дедлайном," and the stand "свёрстан не просто чтобы что-то показать[ —] он свёрстан уже на текущем нашем понимании требовани[й] рынка… с чем JVL хотел[о] бы зайти и начать инсталляции на локациях" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

### Three setups on the stand

Klimovich's narrative for the stand: **(1)** Flex-IQ with Flex-Ports, operated by a cashier; **(2)** the slim kiosk; **(3)** the **Flex recycler** with one machine and one Flex-Port, "скорее всего, настроенный под… техасский софт игровой." Val pulled the recycler out to a meeting of its own — "я даже предлагаю разговор по [рисайклеру] перевести на другой митинг" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

**The name was corrected on the spot, twice.** Klimovich called the second setup "слим[-]пей"; Val: "не слым пэй, а **пей слэм**," defining it as a Flex-Pay slim configuration *(the transcript renders that definition as "машина flack spary allen конфигурация"; the derived notes read it as "a Flex-Pay slim configuration")*, and asked for the document to be changed ("Поменяйте, пожалуйста") — Klimovich: "я в процессе." So the product is **Pay Slim**, a Flex-Pay slim configuration, not "Slim Pay" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]. Its peripheral list, dictated by Val deliberately for the transcript: **bill acceptor, optical reader, card dispenser** and — Klimovich prompting, Val confirming — a **TITO-format printer** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

### The topology, and a new classification

Flex-IQ sits on the counter at the cashier with **its own TITO printer**; it is connected to the gaming machines, **each fitted with a Flex-Port in its current touchscreen version**; IQ and ports hang off **one and the same router**; and the IQ "выступа[е]т в роли gateway к нашему клауд[у] и [и]волят[-]серверу" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

Klimovich introduced a split JVL had not been using: "Мы будем теперь делить наши решения на self[-]сервис, где игрок сам себя обслуживает[,] и на каш[ирные] [«кашель» in the transcript], где требуется [location attendant], чтобы участвовать в процессе… [ре]дем[а] или выпуска тикетов… Вот IQ — это cashier[-]operated решение" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

### The flow, and one capability explicitly not required for the show

Klimovich walked it as the show demo: the cashier creates an **empty wallet** and hands it over; the player loads cash straight into the bill validator on the machine, plays, and at collect presents the wallet through the port, which credits it; he moves to the next machine, loads credits from the wallet, plays, collects again; then returns to the Flex-IQ for redemption and card activation, which runs the **barcode reading flow JVL already has** and needs no fundamental change. The alternative opening is a **loaded** wallet — the cashier takes, say, $20, keys the amount into the IQ and issues a wallet already carrying it — and at redemption a player may instead ask to **top the wallet up** rather than redeem [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

**Splitting** is the one new market-driven capability named, and Klimovich flagged it as optional before describing it: "это **не must-have** к выставке. Это желательно успеть." What it is: at redemption, with the wallet's balance visible, "из моего баланса $100 — 80 загрузим на карточку, [а] на 20… хочу вернуться, продолжить играть," or "80 [на] карточку, 20 отдайте мне… лотерейными [билетами]" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

### The Flex-IQ change list for the show

- **Card swipe becomes barcode scanning.** Pavel: "Сейчас мы делаем свайп карты[ —] а мы меняем это просто на сканирование баркода, да[?]" Klimovich: "Баркода" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].
- **Cashier and master PIN login is unchanged.** Chudinov: "мы ж генерируем пинкоды[ —] один для кассира, один для мастера[?]" Klimovich: "я бы пока это не менял, это вроде звучит довольно логично" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].
- **Self-service has no cashier, so no cashier PIN there**; the **player** PIN is a separate block of changes — Val: "пинкод для игрока… должен быть общий"; Klimovich: "это отдельно у нас блок есть изменений" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].
- **Receipts print automatically, and the PIN becomes a configurable parameter set off** — "Пин[-]код становится конфигурируемым параметр[ом], то есть он off. И… если мы не успеваем… сделать его конфигурируемым к выставке, то давайте просто захардкодим его выключение… Он создаёт лишний фрикшн." Val saw no problem ("я не вижу в этом проблем[ы]") and Pavel agreed it is not a hard task. *(Same direction as the [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-pin-and-wallet-load-configurable-per-location.md|10:02 per-location PIN ruling]], reached two hours earlier and from the show angle rather than the SAS angle.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- **Chudinov's read of the work:** "по флекс[-]айкью я бы ничего криминального не вижу[:] нам разработать новые [UI-]интерфейсы и перенести реализацию с flexpay на flex IQ" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

### Pay Slim — the open item and the schedule

"Главный здесь вопрос — это инсталляция кард[-]диспенсера в слим[-]шасси, слим[-]киоск… главное изменение — это нужен новый форм[-]фактор для… кард[-]диспенсера." Everything else overlaps the Flex-IQ work: "У IQ и у всех self-service киоск[ов]… UX… пересе[кается] процентов на 80, может[,] с небольшими отличиями" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

Schedule, worked out live: Genadi has done the internal brackets but has **not** sent the drawings ("Нет ещё"), and will within "одного[-]2 дней. Сегодня[,] завтра." Val converted that into a plan: "будем говорить, что **в среду чертежи уйдут**. Это значит, что [завод] начнёт **в понедельник** это делать… Мы это отправляем самолётом, значит, **в течение 2 недель это будет в Джорджии**." Genadi separately put the Slim itself at about two weeks. *(The factory's name is rendered "марафайч" in the transcript and is not resolvable — not asserted. Genadi's own timing is transcribed as "в течение 23 недель… 2 недели"; the derived notes read it as about two weeks.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]

### Resourcing, and the one question that was deferred

Val asked who would do the Flex-IQ and Flex-Pay development, and how the work is broken down — "сначала… нужно проработать всякие [capabilities]… потом это нужно разделить на fe[a]t[u]r[e]s." Pavel named **"Дима, Сергей… Антон Серёгин… [и] Владимир [Самусенко]"**, put the UI work at "буквально один день работы, вряд ли больше," and said the main effort falls on the Flex-Port: "основная работа будет именно на флекспорт." *(The derived notes spell the fourth name **Vladimir Samusenko**; the transcript garbles it. "Дима" is used in this call for [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dmitri Chudinov]], who is present and addressed as Дима — whether the resourcing "Дима" is the same person is not stated, and this "Сергей" is not resolvable to a known JVL Sergei. Queued per §4, not force-matched.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]

[[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] asked the obvious follow-up — "А кто будет флекс[-]порт[ом] заниматься?" — and Pavel deferred it: "Это наш следующий митинг будет обсуждать… [до] через один" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear — product, device and schedule only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 7 2026 (9:08) — the Monday the ports went wireless

The Monday's **second and third** meetings, back to back, both Russian, both organised by Val — the morning opened with Klimovich's [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-gacs-show-setup-flex-iq-flex-ports-and-the-e-wallet.md|8:00 show-setup call]] above, which Val closed by noting the next meeting started in ten minutes [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]. Then the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-wifi-on-flex-port-and-qr-provisioning.md|9:08–9:55 Wi-Fi session]] (Val, [[wiki/people/evgeniy-alekseev.md|Evgeniy Alekseev]], [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Andrei Bosik]], [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]], [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]]) and the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-07-sas-aft-on-flex-port-and-remote-update.md|10:02–10:33 SAS/AFT engineers' alignment]] (Val, Pavel, Alekseev, [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dmitri Chudinov]], Klimovich). Val closed the Wi-Fi session saying he would see them in five minutes, and closed the SAS/AFT one promising to send "транскр[ипты] на все митинги[,] то что прошли сегодня" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].

This is the Monday the [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-port-the-smib-sas-module-into-the-flex-port-main-branch.md|Sept-4 SAS decision]] pointed at. What landed, and what did not, is annotated on that decision page.

Rulings from the first meeting: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-wi-fi-module-on-flex-port-with-an-external-router.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-wi-fi-provisioning-by-encrypted-qr.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-every-jvl-device-must-show-what-it-sees.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-andrei-bosik-drives-the-wi-fi-work-and-the-process-chain.md]].

**The architectural shift in one line:** the Flex-Ports stop needing cable to reach the Flex-IQ. A Wi-Fi module is soldered onto the Flex-Port board at roughly **$4–5 per board**; a commercial router hidden in the gaming area is the access point; Flex-IQ and every port talk to it over Wi-Fi; cable stays supported and must still be tested. This **reverses the Aug-24 "Вай фай не пройдёт"** position taken for the Flex-Port-Mini ([[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-flex-port-mini-via-second-sas-only.md]]) — treated as evolution, with the two-week-old objections (antenna per model, a central point that must see every machine) answered rather than ignored [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]].

**It does not replace the cell modem.** The Wi-Fi is the in-premises link; the site uplink is still the LTE modem of [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-21-cell-modem-on-every-machine-from-september-1.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-north-america-modem-variant-and-ask-kore-for-the-apn.md]]. Val leaned on the modem precisely to justify sending install telemetry home — "сейчас у нас уже стоит, у нас будет [LTE] модем" — and neither Sept-7 source proposes Wi-Fi as an answer to the Georgia "7455" failure [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]].

Two board consequences worth keeping here rather than on the decision page: **Flex-Port drops to one serial connector** (Genadi: "нам 3 сериальных порта не нужно… на порте, то есть один можно убрать"), which frees both space and the UART the Wi-Fi module needs; and the printer UART is spare in this variant because there is no printer (Pavel: "поскольку нам принтер не нужен в этом варианте"). Genadi confirmed he had already sent the SAS arrangement for the board — "мы уже договорились, куда сас[,] я уже… послал уже" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_WiFi_Module_on_FlexPorts_and_Provisioning-September-7-2026.md]].

## Sept 7 2026 (mid-morning) — SAS closes the product, and the Flex-IQ punch list gets chased

Rulings: [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-flex-port-goes-sas-and-jvl-drops-the-old-machine-segment.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-pin-and-wallet-load-configurable-per-location.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-remote-firmware-update-mandatory-and-taken-off-yavorsky.md]], [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-flex-port-flash-cut-to-two-sixteen-megabyte-blocks.md]], and — spanning both meetings — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-error-recovery-and-non-functional-requirements-come-first.md]] and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-virtual-test-system-tries-full-arm-emulation-first.md]].

**The commercial line Val drew:** "проект флекс[-]порт сда[ё]тся с[о] [SAS]. Мы уходим от этого гибкого подключения, и мы не будем адресовать ту часть рынка, которая сегодня стоит на старых машинах" — the flexible bill-acceptor/counter connection is dropped and the old-machine segment is conceded. **No number is attached to that segment on this call**, so the standing 15–20 % / 10 % / 90 % contradiction below is not advanced by these sources [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].

**Georgia runs AFT, not TITO** — Chudinov asked, Pavel confirmed, and named the cost: "это другие команды, не те, которые были реализованы… в тестовой прошивке — там поддерживался режим тит[о]… а нам сейчас надо реализовать режим AFT." Pavel had already run the experiment on the Friday, adding AFT to the SAS firmware "которую Евгений, 2 Евгения делали," and wants it finished so the minimal command set can be documented [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].

**Work split, changed from Sept 4:** Pavel takes the larger part of the SAS/AFT implementation himself, because Alekseev is on vacation this week and has the Wi-Fi work, and SAS can be parallelised locally "потому что у нас несколько машин и несколько портов" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].

### The Flex-IQ business Klimovich closed the call with

- **The barcode-activation build promised for Monday is ready.** Chudinov: "Готова[,] сегодня залил" — built and uploaded that day. Klimovich asked for the link; Chudinov said he had checked it on his own terminal and it works, but preferred staging "потому что… там более правдивые данные будут," and someone would send the link. This partly discharges the Sept-2 Flex-IQ punch list ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-activate-incomm-cards-by-barcode-on-flex-iq.md]]) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].
- **The Dwolla transaction went through but has not been seen landing.** Pavel: "этот транзакцию мы видели, что прошла от [Дволы]?" Klimovich: "Да… Деньги снялись. Надо взглянуть на стороне банка, что к нам пришли эти деньги — это я уже не вижу… это наверно [Нэйтан] должен увидеть" — see [[wiki/people/nathan-carver.md|Nathan Carver]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].
- **Why Klimovich was pressing on the screen changes.** "Нам нужна IQ[,] [Ин]ком[у] отправить… видео и скриншоты полного флоу[,] для Того, чтобы они запрувили наше устройство" — InComm needs video and screenshots of the full flow to approve the device (see [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-26-certify-only-flex-iq-and-flex-port-with-incomm.md]]). Chudinov thought the screen work was probably not in this build — "по экранам… не знаю[,] Сергей, может, ещё не успел" — and Pavel undertook to find out what remained, thinking it should finish that day. *(This "Сергей" is not resolvable to a known JVL Sergei from context — queued per §4, not force-matched.)* Klimovich also asked about receipt printing after card activation and got no answer [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].
- **MudaGoose, left open on purpose.** Chudinov asked whether the whole flow still makes sense "если мы уходим от системы [Муда]г[уз]"; Klimovich put it to Val — "мы хотим всё-таки установиться ещё на каких-то локациях [на MudaGoose] хотя бы сейчас?" Val: "Не думаю. Но давайте с вами[,] Андрей[,] ещё подумаем день или 2 и обсудим. Завтра я буду уже на месте," adding "только напомните мне, я забыл." Left open [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear across both meetings — component costs ($4–5 Wi-Fi module, $148 chip) and vendor selection only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 8 2026 (Dixie) — removing the bill acceptor, put to an operator again

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-08-dixie-amusement-bundle-offer-and-card-economics.md|Sept-8 Dixie visit]] [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] floated stripping the **bill acceptor out of the game machine entirely** — cash goes into the kiosk, the printed ticket becomes the wallet, the player scans it at the machine. *(Corrected on back-fill: not the first customer test — on Aug 25 Val told Epic "the best scenario is no printer, no bill acceptor", and Kenny Patel replied "It's not going to work in Georgia"; see Aug 25 (Epic) above [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Epic_Amusement_Kenny_Patel_Visit-August-25-2026.md]].)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Georgia_Transactional_Equipment_and_Bundle_Economics-September-8-2026.md]]

- **The pitch:** "**You will come in. You put money in here, twenty dollars. It will print out the ticket. You will scan it, and that's it. Your ticket now is your wallet.**" And the commercial case: "**You don't need a bill acceptor. You could save a lot of money.**"
- **Val named the one real cost himself, unprompted:** "the only thing I see as an inconvenience is that **when you have a bill acceptor at the machine and you want to add money, that's convenient. Really convenient. When you are sitting, you are in that rush. You're zen.**"
- **[[wiki/people/salim-khambawala.md|Salim]]'s objection is behavioural, not technical — the session timer.** "people are normally, when the game ends, **that thirty-second timer or sixty-second timer comes on**, people are fit… **So getting up and going to the kiosk and getting there and the timer starts and you are in the [trouble]. So that's one.** The reason they don't want to get up every time and go to the [kiosk]." His framing: "you put pros on one. **Excellent idea. Get rid of the [cash]. Zero [cash]. Scan.** And when it got out, there is some negative side of it… [if the cons] **overweigh** the pros, then it's not okay."
- **And adoption is a two-sided education problem:** "**It's all about educating them. As a location, and then educate the player, and then its adoption from both of those pieces.**" His evidence that adoption is slow: "I still have those **wooden cabinets. I think ten of them still in my route**… **some of their customers don't even want that screen. They just want two buttons.**" On the longer arc: "**Maybe three years, five years down the road, it will become the next model.**"
- **A JVL voice in the room proposed settling it by experiment, not argument:** "**You start with certain locations. You run [A/B] tests. You see how people behave, and then you start adopting the best practices, step by step. There is no question that you should roll out this solution.**" Recorded as an open item on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-08-the-bundle-put-to-dixie-with-installation-explicitly-excluded.md]]; **nobody ruled on it.**
- **Salim's counter-model, offered as where this ends anyway:** face recognition. "**You don't even need a scanning to become your player card. They have a camera, your face very easy**… once you walk into the machine, **your face is your wallet**." Val's summary of the whole category: "**None of it is [unknown] technology. It's only legislation.**" Salim: "**And technology always runs faster than the law.**"

**§15.8:** clear — device configuration and market-adoption content only; no named-person compensation.

## Sept 9 2026 — the programme gets a date, a bottleneck and a staging rule

Two meetings the same morning ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-september-resource-plan-and-flex-port-readiness.md|7:02 AM]] Russian with Minsk, [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-october-rollout-staging-and-unit-ramp.md|8:43 AM]] English in Florida) turn the August–September device work into a dated rollout. The rulings live on their own pages; what belongs to the product architecture is:

- **Flex-Port is the bottleneck of the whole transactional line.** [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]]: "пока не сделается какой[-]то функционал в[о] флекспорте[,] дальше проверить на flexpay какой[-]то функционал будет невозможно" — FlexPay functions cannot be verified until the matching Flex-Port function exists. Readiness best case **Sept 25**. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-flex-port-readiness-slips-to-september-25-and-stays-the-bottleneck.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]
- **A location gets FlexPay *or* Flex-IQ in the first iteration, never both** — "на лакейшн нельзя поставить айкью плюс flexpay"; both together is iteration two, with Wi-Fi. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-one-transactional-unit-per-location-until-the-wi-fi-iteration.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]
- **The October-1 target configuration:** FlexPay with bill acceptor, card dispenser and TITO printer, running self-service, the wallet generated before or after play, the payout printed at the FlexPay, and an LTE modem at the location on the machine and on the Pay or IQ. Both devices are built on the **RK3568** and its modems are confirmed working: "мы и флекспей и флекс IQ будем делать именно на р[]к." [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-first-transactional-installation-planned-for-october-1.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Three Flex-Port features added to capability 165** — an optional PIN, reading the machine's SAS flags (door open, machine blocked) so the port disables itself, and remote log retrieval, because today logs come off only on a USB stick. The **wallet transfer** becomes its own capability because it reaches the server side. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-three-more-flex-port-features-onto-capability-165.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_September_Resource_Plan_and_FlexPort_Readiness-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Everything is SAS, on wires, through October and probably November** — one wire per device, run in parallel, with all ports connected "only to the second fastboard" (Val's wording; the Russian call says "к сас 2"). Wi-Fi is second in urgency and is the piece to sacrifice. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-wi-fi-is-second-in-urgency-and-october-november-run-wires.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_October_Rollout_Staging_and_Unit_Ramp-September-9-2026.md]]
- **Hardware readiness as stated:** brackets done and sent to the manufacturer; 100 plastics, 20 hardware, unlimited plastic for the Pay unit, about 100 screens, boards in redesign arriving in about two weeks; the printer arriving installed is "a question mark" with a configuration swap as the fallback. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-the-mould-without-the-screen-comes-first-and-the-printer-has-a-fallback.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_October_Rollout_Staging_and_Unit_Ramp-September-9-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — device architecture, component stock and unit counts only.

## Sept 9 2026 (evening) — the Flex-Port described to a customer as an AFT device, and the kiosk's spec stated as no-cash-no-recycling

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-09-arc-gaming-backend-displacement-negotiation.md|4:35 PM ARC meeting]] a JVL colleague gave the **plainest customer-facing description of what a Flex-Port is** anywhere in the corpus, and attached the exclusivity condition to it as a property of the hardware rather than a commercial term: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]

> "It's an **AFT device**. Think of it as a **slot AFT device. It fits into an AFT window. It connects to SAS two port. So we emulate an AFT device. But we must be exclusive in the location. Otherwise, it doesn't work for us.**"

*(Recorded as spoken. "SAS two port" is the transcript's rendering; the derived notes read it as **the SAS 2 port** — the same second-SAS-port dependency tracked in the open contradiction below.)* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]

**And the kiosk, spec'd to a customer in one sentence** — by [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]], and explicitly *against* what a cash-handling kiosk does: [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]

> Sloan: "Without recycling." — Val: "**No cash dispensing.** It's an **e wallet, bill acceptor**… **card dispenser, and a printer. But it's an e wallet you could move from machine to machine.**"

He described the unit's form factor as "a **slinkyiosk** that fits between the machines" *(transcribed as written; the derived notes read it as a **slim** unit)*, and the port arithmetic as "**five ports plus our machine, so six right now.
