You have six on average.**" **This is consistent with the no-recycler posture already recorded** on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-hold-the-recycler-back-but-sign-no-undertaking.md]] and with the Sept-8 Dixie bill-acceptor discussion above — **but note it is stated here as a settled product fact to a customer, not as an internal position.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Backend_Displacement_Negotiation-September-9-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — device architecture and configuration only.

## Sept 11 2026 — October 1 narrows to one product, and the device family gets a shared-components argument

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-transactional-mvp1-scope-and-priorities.md|Sept-11 8:04 AM call]] is where the October-1 configuration is finally pinned. The rulings live on their own pages; what belongs to the product architecture is:

- **October 1 ships Flex IQ, not FlexPay.** Two days after the Sept-9 plan named FlexPay in self-service, [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] ruled the opposite way round: one configuration, Flex IQ, with the **Flex-Port unaffected** and FlexPay moved to MVP 2. It is conditional on the Flex-Port bottleneck being real — "если этого [ботлнека] нету, тогда мы пересмотрим M[V]P один." [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-mvp1-goes-with-flex-iq-alone-and-one-kiosk-per-location.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **The MVP 1 device rule, restated unchanged:** "MVP один работает через линкед[ ]этот самый нетворк… [и] на лакейш[н]е может стоять один киоск, либо IQ, либо flexpay" — the linked network, one kiosk per location. Same as [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-one-transactional-unit-per-location-until-the-wi-fi-iteration.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].
- **Card limits are now stated as product facts:** a **$1,000 maximum per card**, and an inherited minimum that auto-fails anything under about $5 or $10 and no longer works with the portal. A payout above the card maximum issues **one card at $1,000 and leaves the remainder on the player's ticket / e-wallet**, with the existing receipt logic printing both amounts. Splitting across several cards was proposed by Klimovich, rejected, and parked on MVP 2. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-one-card-at-a-thousand-dollars-and-the-rest-stays-on-the-ticket.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **Bill escrow is kiosk-only and leaves MVP 1; the player PIN leaves with it and Flex IQ is PIN-free** because the cashier operates it — the hook stays, defaulted off. **Remote update stays in MVP 1, remote config goes to MVP 2** (nothing defined to configure yet), Flex-IQ software/firmware management joins MVP 1, and the **Flex-Port master-device** half of remote firmware update is pulled into MVP 1. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-mvp1-boundary-bill-escrow-and-pin-out-remote-update-and-master-device-in.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]
- **Blocking a location now reaches the ports.** Lease expiry set by hand in the hub stops Flex IQ, FlexPay **and the Flex-Ports**, so players cannot play; automatic blocking is never enabled. [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-lease-blocking-reaches-the-flex-ports-and-is-never-automatic.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]

**The shared-components argument, made for the first time as a product-build problem.** [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Andrey Bosik]] put it plainly: the Flex devices and the future ones share "очень много общей части" — Wi-Fi, screens, the QR-code scanner, remote update — and "[п]лата рк она везде одна и та же[;] … вай фай[,] LT[E], он будет абсолютно одинаково на всех этих устройствах[,] вообще идентично." The consequence he objects to: "когда надо будет в каком[-]то одном поменять[,] … мы сразу во всех хотим это менять… [а] сейчас это получается в каждой репозитории… поменя[ть]." [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]: "[п]олностью поддерживаю", and on the UI specifically, "сама логика экранов[,] их дизайн должна быть унифицированная для всех устройств под разное разрешение." Val pointed at [[wiki/people/dmitri-kovalchuk.md|Dmitri Kovalchuk]] as the man who knows the subject and predicted he would propose doing it **as a component**. Bosik's closing correction is the important one: his point is broader than screens — "я про сборку… продуктов и про внесение этих изменений, чтобы не в каждой репозитории заходить… а ты в одном месте поменял[,] и все продукты эти изменения за собой подтянули." No decision was taken; it was flagged as not this meeting's topic. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]

**Machine compatibility is named as the live MVP-1 risk.** [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]]: Flex-Port compatibility "будет с какими[-]то моделями машин… имплементировано, а с какими[-]то нет", may need different logic per type, and may need per-cabinet work landing even after MVP 2. Klimovich agreed they hit it in MVP 1 already and set the only mitigation available: find out as early as possible which machines sit at the first installation locations. **The Georgia office has no non-JVL cabinets**, so he is arranging with **master licensees** ("с некоторыми мастерами") either to visit a warehouse with many different machines or to have several machines lent to the JVL warehouse — a window of **one to two days maximum**, in which they fit a Flex-Port, run a quick compatibility test and move it across as many machines as possible. "[К] этому моменту у нас должно быть всё условно готово для тестирования." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]] See [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]].

**And the first installation itself is a mixed-manufacturer location.** [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dmitri Chudinov]] asked whether the first install would be on JVL machines or other people's; Klimovich: "[в]се, какие будут на локации… мы устанавливаемся на реальную локацию[;] там будет… как минимум одна машина [JVL,] но и машины других производителей" *(the transcript renders the manufacturer as "GL")* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].

**How a release actually reaches a device today**, described by Bosik when Val asked how staging works: a GitHub Action creates an artefact that lands on Amazon, and a web interface to the transactional terminal-management system carries "кнопка… выбрать[,] какую версию обновления мы хотим залить, на какое устройство." A demo was offered and Val declined — he had another meeting starting [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].

**The in-house rehearsal before October 1, made concrete.** Val asked how they practise before installing anything — "нам в офисе нужно это собрать, поставить, посмотреть, чтоб это работало[,] потренироваться… какой[-]то… ин[-]хаус практис." Pavel: the Georgia office already has assembled Flex IQ units, so it is a matter of installing the new software, connecting the **TITO printer** and moving the **QR scanner** — a job [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi]] should control. Flex-Port software is released as functionality is added, and **[[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth]] can already flash if needed**; Chudinov expects a simpler route once the first build is out, updating the Georgia terminals automatically [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]. This is the office-rehearsal requirement Val set on Sept 9 ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-first-transactional-installation-planned-for-october-1.md]]) turning into named tasks.

**Weekend work is paid, and Val wants it offered to everyone.** Pavel proposed Chudinov work Saturday and Sunday on the services needed for Flex-Port / Flex-IQ communication and card activation on the e-wallet path, "не за… выходной день, а за дополнительные деньги". Val agreed and widened it: "я бы хотел бы, чтобы все работали все дни [за] дополнительные деньги", called a schedule that needs only one person wrong — "это значит[,] неправильно составлен график[;] нужны все, нужно всё акселер[ировать]" — while conceding "я людей заставить не могу работать" and confirming "безусловно, я за это буду платить." Pavel also took the weekend, on Flex-Port and on the bill-validator question. **Zhenya and Sasha are unavailable**, and Sasha's tasks are FlexPay, which MVP 1 no longer needs. Val closed: "с сегодняшнего дня до 15 декабря[,] у нас очень много вещей, которые нужны" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]]. *(The "Zhenya" here is resolved by §4 context to [[wiki/people/eugine-yavorski.md|Eugine Yavorski]] — the FlexPort/FlexPay integration-software Zhenya, and the one Irina named earlier in the same call as owning the machine-side money-out capabilities through the port. Flagged as a context resolution, not asserted from the source. "Sasha" is [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Alexander Biratka]], absent that day — see the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-11-nova-game-change-regulatory-procedure.md|9:30 call]].)*

**A side note on Wi-Fi coverage, from before the meeting proper.** Bosik was working out whether Wi-Fi reaches across a location. Klimovich described a typical Georgia site: a petrol station with four machines by the wall in the retail area and another five around a corner behind a wall, all counted as **one location**, with about **five metres** between points and walls in between — "[з]аходите в заправочную станцию… около стенки стоят там 4 аппарата[,] и потом вы проходите вглубь… за угол[,] за стенку[,] и там ещё 5 аппаратов… [и] это всё считается одна локация"; "[м]етров 5"; "[с]тенки есть, бывают стенки, это надо учитывать" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Scope_and_Priorities-September-11-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear — device architecture, scope and working-pattern policy only; no rate or amount is named for any person.

## Sept 14 2026 — the MVP 1 use cases walked end to end, and the product finally has an answer for a ticket that never printed

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-14-transactional-mvp1-use-cases-and-ewallet-ticket-recovery.md|Sept-14 8:01 AM call]] is the first session that describes MVP 1 as a *player experience* rather than a capability list. [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] opened by declaring it "полностью сформирован на уровне [capability]" with only the UI/UX screens and final texts outstanding. The rulings live on their own pages; what belongs to the product architecture is:

**MVP 1 restated in one line and four scenarios.** "[MVP] один для нас это IQ, работающий в [environment] flexport. Никаких пока киосков, никаких [self-service] систем." The four framing scenarios: money reaches an e-wallet through the counter, so IQ must be able to create a loaded wallet; money moves from the wallet to the machine through the port; **if the location is blocked — "я специально не подчёркиваю, каким образом, каким механизмом" — creating a loaded wallet on IQ must not work**; and no transfer may lose money [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]. The device scope is unchanged from [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-mvp1-goes-with-flex-iq-alone-and-one-kiosk-per-location.md]].

**The port must talk to the player, not just move money.** Scanning a carrier on Flex-Port checks the balance automatically, but the transfer is not automatic: "[м]ы должны показать игроку его баланс[,] и не сразу переносить деньги автоматом." [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] gave it the framing everyone adopted — "то есть диалог должен произойти между игроком и портом" — and Klimovich agreed: "нам надо подтверждение от игрока… и часть этого диалога[ —] это показать ему актуальный баланс" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]].

**Every stored balance is a snapshot — stated as a global requirement.** "[Е]сли у нас есть на любом из [gateway]… snapshot баланса [e-]valleta… то это просто снэпшот. **Каждый раз, когда мы делаем что[-]либо с балансом, мы обязаны получать актуальное состояние баланса.** Это такое глобальное… требование, которое регулирует всю логику оперирования с [e-]волитами" — otherwise a wallet recorded at $20 may since have become $5 "а мы продолжаем оперировать, как будто их там 20" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]].

**E-wallet lifetime, and the carrier rule.** A TTL is introduced — 30 days by default, per-location, 1 to 365 — and the ticket prints the validity period rather than only the creation date. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-ewallet-ttl-defaults-to-thirty-days-and-is-set-per-location.md]]. It supersedes the April-2025 open question on this page about a 30-day versus ~24-hour wallet window.

**The collect fork, and the failure it creates.** Pressing collect now puts Flex-Port into a fork — scan your carrier, or have IQ print a new ticket — and the wallet is created at the button press, before the print, so a dead printer cannot lose the money. The cashier recovers it from the transaction list. Whether that recovery is a reissue or a plain reprint is parked. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-the-ewallet-is-created-before-its-ticket-prints.md]]. The port's present behaviour was measured by Klimovich himself: on collect the machine goes to zero immediately, the money waits on the port, and the timeout is "что[-]то в районе 30 секунд" before it drops into unclaimed [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]].

**Storage stays local, and so does reporting.** Requirement 1538 moves off capability 158 to a new MVP 2 cloud capability; the MVP 1 dashboard shows activated cards only. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-mvp1-stays-local-and-the-cloud-balance-requirement-moves-to-mvp2.md]].

**Redemption gets its floor, and the play-through rule is rescued.** Below the card minimum plus the activation fee, MVP 1 refuses and hands the ticket back — no lottery-ticket fallback — and the deposited/playable dual balance must show everywhere, cashier screen included. Recorded as the Sept-14 update on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-one-card-at-a-thousand-dollars-and-the-rest-stays-on-the-ticket.md]].

**InComm's fraud controls are InComm's.** "[Д]ля каждой локации у них есть понятие [velocity] и [daily] cap. Мы этим не управляем, и более того, мы даже скорее всего не знаем, какие есть значения для каждой локации." Klimovich's question to the engineers was whether a cap rejection returns an explicit error he can translate for the cashier ("ваша локация достигла day cap, свяжитесь с [InComm]") or just a generic failure. [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]]: everything InComm documents is handled and interpreted — "всё, что у них в документации ошибки… мы их все обрабатываем и как[-]то интерпретируем" — and a bare "error" on screen means the response could not be recognised. He returns interpretations, not codes: "я возвращаю не ошибки, я возвращаю интерпретацию… [например,] с этой карточкой что[-]то не в порядке[,] используйте другую[,] это уже активирован[а]", with the raw codes logged. [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]] asked whether those interpretations reach Spanish; they do, from a hard-coded collection — "[з]ашито конечно… мы ещё не попали на следующий уровень" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]. See [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]].

**The printer becomes a first-class device.** A receipt prints automatically on card activation; a broken or empty printer does not block card issue; and the printer reports back. [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] offered the machine-side tilt list and [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] confirmed it is the same printer — "они точно такие же будут[,] и там принтер такой же" — covering low paper and no paper (Irina), a head error and a general error ("[о]шибка головы… [д]женерал ошибка", Pavel), and a jam (named by Klimovich in the question). He wants IQ to show them **and to warn before the paper runs out**: "он должен превентивно кидать сообщение на экране IQ, что там заканчивается бумага[,] перезаряди кассету", and asked for the list of tilts JVL can handle [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]].

**Val's own requirement — preventive supply telemetry — is accepted and deferred to MVP 2.** "[М]ы должны измерять количество бумаги… в любом промежутке времени[,] и количество карточек… и насколько у нас забит [stacker]… [э]ти вещи… постоянно должны [в] предупредительном режиме быть [available,] и мы должны [передавать] эту информацию оператору или… администратору." Biratka: "у нас, наверное, нет таких к[а]пабилити пока что." Klimovich: "[э]то учтено для [MVP] 2[;] как только у нас появляется киоск, мы это всё уже должны будем уметь делать." Val accepted that it is noted [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]].

**Two cashiers, two IQs — not supported, and not wanted.** [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]] asked whether the scenarios assume exactly one Flex IQ per location. Klimovich: "давай не будем сейчас про это думать[;] мы не можем этого поддерживать сейчас, и я не очень понимаю, когда нам это может понадобиться[;] кассир чаще всего один", with the IQ-plus-kiosk version of the ecosystem named as where it would be solved. Irina: "дорогое удовольствие… бессмысленная" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]. This is the one-gateway-per-location assumption of [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-one-transactional-unit-per-location-until-the-wi-fi-iteration.md]] holding under a direct challenge.

**Top-up on IQ — a capability with no use case.** Irina pointed out the wallet can also be topped up at the counter. Klimovich conceded the gap in the use-case set and closed it against the capabilities: "[т]акого [use] кейса нету[;] действительно, я забыл его упомянуть… [но] опция добавить, обновить баланс на IQ должна быть точно так же, как и создать [e-]волит на IQ." Irina noted capabilities **148, 149 and 150** had been treated as already implemented, needing only automated tests; Chudinov confirmed the business logic came over from FlexPay and works, "но если [она] поменяется, то и туда что[-]то подправлять." Klimovich's answer is that the UI changes regardless — "на IQ не было такого UI в принципе", and FlexPay's is self-service facing a player where IQ's faces a cashier, so "другой объём информации, и попадание в этот интерфейс тоже по другой логике происходит" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]. *(Irina also asked Chudinov about **capability 115**, which she said he is working on inside MVP 1; the surrounding exchange is too garbled to record what was concluded about it.)*

**The capability list, checked against itself with Claude.** Pavel's verdict on the use cases was "всё очевидно", and on the capability list: "огромная часть из них выглядит как почти сделано, или сделанное, но не покрыт[о]… автотестами", mostly needing corrections and some added reports. Klimovich had run the whole list through Claude in both directions the previous day — use cases against capabilities, then capabilities back to the experience they produce — "я его вчера гонял и туда и сюда во все стороны с Клодом… посмотри, всё ли учтено[;] потом мы прогнали в обратную сторону" — and considers it current [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]. Chudinov's complaint about the same tool closing a capability against a cloud requirement is on the MVP-2 page above.

**One overlap Pavel found and Klimovich kept deliberately.** "[C]apabilit[y] 171 [location block enforce]… похоже, что это то же самое, что… terminal lease and remote disable[, capability] 118." Klimovich agreed — "я соглашусь, что 118… перекликается очень сильно со 171" — but kept 171 to carry the detailed requirements: how a block affects the port, IQ and the different kiosk types separately, what is shown on screen, what state things sit in. He remembered one rule he had written: **"если операция была в mid стадии какой[-]то, и в этот момент прилетела блокировка локации, то операция как минимум должна быть завершена"** — an in-flight operation must at least be allowed to finish [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]. Lease blocking reaching the ports was settled on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-lease-blocking-reaches-the-flex-ports-and-is-never-automatic.md]].

**SAS stops being JVL's own invention.** Klimovich learned on this call that the funds-transfer screens appear on third-party machines too because the standard says so, and Val ruled that capabilities are written against the published Gaming Standards Association SAS standard, targeting 6.02. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-capabilities-are-written-against-the-published-sas-standard.md]].

**Process, at both ends of the meeting.** Manual build download-and-install is killed outright with a 30-day target ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-nobody-downloads-and-installs-builds-thirty-day-target.md]]), and the Jira status chain is tightened with an automated exception report ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-nothing-in-mvp1-may-still-be-proposed-and-bosik-writes-the-exception-report.md]]). The one MVP-1 report — cards issued, for a Georgia Lottery inspection — is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-one-report-must-make-mvp1-cards-issued-for-a-georgia-lottery-inspection.md]]. The meeting closed with the next one set for the following morning at the same time, to walk the UI/UX.

**§15.8:** clear — product design, standards and Jira process only; no rate, amount or performance judgement for any named person appears in either source.

## Sept 14 2026 (12:04 PM) — the hardware side of the same day: Slim drawings out, a mechanical card-level sensor, and only one recycler

Four hours after the MVP-1 use-case walk-through above, Val took the same programme through its **hardware** list with [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]] and [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-14-hardware-priorities-tito-case-and-street-cabinet.md|Sept-14 12:04 PM call]], Russian). Substance that belongs to the device family [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]:

**FlexPay Slim — the first ten are in metal, and the full package lands the same day.** Val's understanding was that the drawings were released and "**китайцы взяли металл в работу с сегодняшнего дня, с понедельника**"; Genadi corrected the date — "**Они взяли в работу в пятницу. Да ну, на прошлой неделе ещё**" — into work the previous **Friday** — and confirmed "**мы должны в пятницу… отправить**". That covers "**эти 10 проект[ных] 10 [units], которые у нас есть**". Genadi was releasing the **full bill of materials and the complete drawing set** that day — "**включая там новые харности для карди[ ]Спенсер**", new card-dispenser harnesses included — so that a request for about **50** more could be issued the next day: "**чтобы в принципе завтра можно было… выпустить реквест, не знаю, 50 [units]**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]. *(The transcript renders the unit word as "**центов**" — literally "cents" — in both the 10 and the 50; the derived notes read it as units, which is the reading taken here and is an **inference**. The 10-unit Slim build is the one tracked from [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-04-flex-slim-first-ten-and-flex-port-sas.md|Sept 4]].)* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]

**The card dispenser must report its level mechanically, not by counting.** Val's requirement: "**нам обязательно нужно иметь возможность знать, какой [card] level… [к]огда стакер заполнен, когда… уровень уменьшается**", and explicitly against the software answer — "**я понимаю, что можно просто считать, какое количество карточек выпущено за какой[-]то период. Но я бы хотел бы иметь другую верификацию — механическую верификацию**." Pavel agreed on the reason: counting "**не гарантирует, что там кто[-]то чего[-]то не достал**" — it does not prove nobody took cards out. He has seen it on at least one dispenser model: a sensor "**выставляется на нужную глубину… если становится 20 карт, он начинает сигнализировать, либо 30, либо 40**" — set to a chosen depth, signalling at **20, 30 or 40 cards** remaining. Val: order the dispensers **with that option**. Pavel's caveat: "**эту опцию надо сейчас проверить… [в] какой[-]то из модели вроде бы не работала[,] этот опрос сенсора**" — the sensor polling did not work on one model and must be re-checked [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]. This is the mechanical half of the **preventive supply telemetry** Val asked for at 8:01 that morning and Klimovich deferred to MVP 2 (above).

**A named dispenser candidate — recorded as rendered, not identified.** Genadi's proposal line is garbled ("**так мы берем просим катек**"); Pavel answered "**Хорошо, проверяем синкотек**" and Genadi added "**Он дешевле и лучше… И там ещё было светится, и это лоу[ ]левел**" — cheaper, better, and it had a low-level indicator. **"синкотек" / "Sinkotek" is the transcript's rendering and no real-world vendor is identified here**; the derived notes carry it with "name uncertain" too. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]

**The recycler: one unit only, and the variant that keeps the dispenser.** A validator had arrived; Val asked where — "**это в Торонто или это [Джорджия], куда он пришёл?**" — and **the transcript does not answer him**; Genadi's reply is garbled apart from "**миша организовывает [ship]м[е]нт в Белоруссию**" ([[wiki/people/mikhail-perelman.md|Mikhail Perelman]] organising the shipment to Belarus), with [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth]] named in the same broken sentence. On fitment into FlexPay Slim [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]:

- **Only one will be ordered, because of its size.** "**мы закажем, для него он очень большой. Мы закажем только… один — там пад[?] будет другой, а всё остальное то же самое.**" *("пади" is garbled; the derived notes read "pad".)*
- **The bill acceptor comes out; the optical sensor and printer stay.** Val: "**бил[ ][акцептор] нам не нужен, мы его забираем, но оптический сенсор остаётся, принтер остаётся**."
- **Whether the card dispenser still fits is unchecked.** "**честно говоря, я не проверял, но если поместится — он только в самом [низу]**" — only at the very bottom, if at all.
- **They take option 2 — the variant that keeps the dispenser.** "**если мы хотим ещё с диспенсером, то мы возьмём 2 вариант**"; Val agreed.
- **Integration starts when it reaches Minsk.** Val: "**когда придёт этот [recycler] в Минск, мы начнём интеграция**."
- **And Pavel brought the one piece of good news on the call:** "**я посмотрел документацию и файлы от вот этого рисайклера. У них есть библиотека для RK, ну в смысле для армов… сэкономит, я думаю, неделю разработки точно**" — the recycler ships an **ARM library**, saving about a week of development.

**The standalone TITO printer enclosure** — external desktop power supply, RS-232 in from outside, back-panel connectors, sized for 400 tickets, no security — is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-tito-printer-cases-come-before-the-street-cabinet.md]], together with the work order that put it in front of the street cabinet. **Firmware update inside MVP 1**, and the rule that Flex IQ and FlexPay update themselves before they flash the Flex-Ports, is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-remote-firmware-update-is-in-mvp1-and-flex-iq-updates-itself-first.md]].

**The Flex IQ rear screen stays alive in MVP 1.** Val to Pavel: "**вам нужно сделать так, чтобы экран всё[-]таки работал… [х]отя бы даже какой[-]то стандарт[ный], что[-]то рисовал**" — it must show *something*, even a standard image. Pavel: that was already the plan — "**там же сейчас промоскрин, что, типа, «вы можете забрать [свои] выигрыши здесь»**" — and "**мы оставим всё остальное, диалог с плеером мы убираем**": the promo message stays, the player dialogue goes. *(Pavel says the message "**будет крутиться**" and then immediately "**но не крутиться там**" — whether it cycles or is static is **not clear in the source** and is not asserted; the derived notes read it as staying on the display.)* This is the same rear screen whose **physical removal** Genadi is separately preparing on the Flex IQ hardware — see below. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]

**Flex IQ mechanical changes — half done.** Val asked where the changer/reader placement change and the rear-screen removal stood. Genadi: [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth]] "**получила 10 крышечек**" — received 10 covers — and "**мы можем 10 штук сделать [manually]… борты уже изменили… поэтому это можно сделать [manually]**" — ten units can be done by hand because the boards are already changed. But "**[э]краны… [с]нять нельзя**" — the screens cannot simply be taken off: "**я должен это ещё сделать на этой неделе… крышку заказать. И сделать перм[анент] чейндж**" — order a cover this week and issue a permanent change. Val: "**Это ещё не сделано.**" Genadi: "**Нет.**" *(Val's own item list here renders as "**эти [changers] по расположению**" and "**[ридора]**"; the derived notes read the second as the reader. Recorded as a **reading**, not asserted.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Hardware_Priorities_TITO_Case_and_Street_Cabinet-September-14-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear — component, drawing and build content only; no named-person compensation appears in either Sept-14 12:04 file.

## Sept 15 2026 — the balance finally gets a name, the screens arrive, and Val puts a date on the requirements that do not exist

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-15-transactional-mvp1-balance-naming-and-ui-flow-walkthrough.md|Sept-15 8:03 AM call]] is the direct sequel to the previous day's use-case session and the first time the product is walked as *screens* rather than as capabilities. It ran 1 hour 41 minutes. The rulings live on their own pages; what belongs to the product architecture is:

**A wallet balance is three lines, and the same three words everywhere.** **Total** = everything on the ticket and the figure the machine loads; **Playable only** = the part that cannot yet be withdrawn; **Redeemable** = the part that can. Playable-only plus redeemable equals total, so the arithmetic on screen is visible and correct — which the previous two-line presentation was not. No separate engineering vocabulary: Klimovich proposed splitting internal terms from player-facing ones and both Val and Biratka refused. The term "playable only" comes from the SAS specification. Internally two balances remain so a failed transfer returns to the side it came from, with withdrawal priority playable-first. Val named it an ADR on the record and required it in the repository. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-balance-is-total-playable-only-and-redeemable-everywhere.md]]. This supersedes the bare two-counter description carried on this page from the Aug-2025 CMS block diagram.

**Promotional money is a fourth kind of money and is out of MVP 1.** It moves machine to machine without becoming redeemable. Val expects it to become live in Georgia "очень, очень скоро" as hand-pays go away, and says JVL intends to push promotional specifics through the Flex-Port acting as an AFT device — "это точно падает в наш… бизнес план". Irina's line held it out of the October-1 release: not needed by the 1st, therefore MVP 2 or 3 [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Balance_Naming_and_UI_Flow_Walkthrough-September-15-2026.md]].

**The Flex-Port idle screen loses its buttons and the scanner never sleeps.** Scan-or-insert-money is the whole idle state; language becomes a top-right service toggle that switches on the fly; error and block states share one screen with different texts, with **maintenance** and **lockout** as two distinct signals per Val. A permanently-live scanner is confirmed safe — JVL's own units have run that way for a year, and motion detection keeps the illumination (the part that wears) off until something approaches. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-flex-port-idle-screen-never-sleeps-and-the-port-mirrors-the-machine.md]].

**The port mirrors the machine's state — including a block JVL did not issue.** The Georgia Lottery locks machines over SAS port 1 without touching JVL's system. Val's first position was "это не моя проблема"; Pavel refused it and Klimovich supplied the ownership argument — JVL owns the e-wallet, so a player who loads money and then cannot play or recover it is JVL's problem — and Val conceded, "вы более гуманитарные люди, чем я". The behaviour already works in the build handed to Klimovich on Sept 14 but is untested with two SAS ports connected.

**A collect timeout must never destroy money.** Klimovich measured current Flex-Port behaviour and found the money evaporates. Val: "игрок никогда не может потерять свои деньги". The agreed shape is an unclaimed-money wallet — created, funded, marked, and simply not printed — with the specification taken from **AFT** plus individual states' unclaimed-property/banking law, sourced first from [[wiki/people/juan-martinez.md|Juan Martinez]] and then [[wiki/organizations/gli.md|GLI]] as a paid consultation. Explicitly not MVP 1. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-a-timeout-sends-the-money-to-unclaimed-and-the-player-never-loses-it.md]].

**The Flex IQ cashier flow is settled end to end** — a four-digit PIN with Cancel removed, and Val's requirement that the login method become selectable between PIN and magnetic stripe card, with the magstripe reader confirmed staying in the hardware (only the second screen is being covered over); a three-route home screen; redemption pre-filling the maximum redeemable and freezing it on continue after a fresh balance check; the activation receipt always printing while top-up receipts become optional; and a records list with date and direction filters. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-flex-iq-cashier-flow-login-redemption-receipts-and-records.md]]. Administrative screens (operator mode, cashier accounts, PIN changes, Wi-Fi setup, diagnostics, connected-port list) are pushed to MVP 2 on the assumption remote update lands.

**Reissue versus reprint is closed as reprint**, against its author, on Chudinov's audit-trail argument — recorded as the Sept-15 update on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-the-ewallet-is-created-before-its-ticket-prints.md]].

**Every device on the floor carries its own LTE modem and SIM.** The location router is not the shared modem; four or five devices at a location are each self-sufficient while still seeing each other over Wi-Fi. Val asked whether the spare modems can back each other up when Wi-Fi drops. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-every-device-carries-its-own-lte-modem-and-sim.md]].

**The MVP 1 cloud scope is pinned to exactly two things** — device/operator online status and the issued-cards dashboard — with billing and invoices continuing as they work today, and machine statistics over SAS named by Val as "наш гвоздь программы" and by Klimovich as a tiered paid package for later MVPs. Recorded as the Sept-15 update on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-mvp1-stays-local-and-the-cloud-balance-requirement-moves-to-mvp2.md]].

**Process.** The Jira link between a capability and a requirement is reversed to "capability requires requirement", and planning dates always sit on the lowest level of work that exists so the roll-up never breaks ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-capability-requires-requirement-and-dates-sit-on-the-lowest-level.md]]). Klimovich proposed making the daily call standing practice and Val accepted.

**And the close.** Val stopped the meeting to demand the non-functional requirements in writing — "если это не написано, этого нету" — named the five engineers who own them, gave over the rest of the week, and set **Monday September 21** as the grooming gate for the whole of MVP 1, functional and non-functional together. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-week-goes-to-non-functional-requirements-and-monday-september-21-is-the-deadline.md]].

**§15.8:** clear — product design, standards, Jira process, network architecture and product-tier economics only; no named person's pay, rate or performance judgement appears in either source.

## Sept 15 2026 (2:24 PM) — the whole flow demonstrated end-to-end to an operator, in English, for the first time in the corpus

Six hours after the balance-naming call above, [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]] ran the e-wallet live in front of a Georgia operator at the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-15-unlimited-amusement-mega-hit-blaze-v3-and-bundle-pitch.md|Unlimited Amusement visit]] — [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] handing it over with "**I will let Andre show you one of the ways. I know that you have concerns. One of the ways that you can operate. There are more ways to operate, but let Andre start somewhere.**" It is the clearest plain-English statement of the product in the corpus, and it is stated as **sales demonstration**, not as spec. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Unlimited_Amusement_Mega_Hit_Blaze_V3_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pitch-September-15-2026.md]]

**The flow as he walked it:** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Unlimited_Amusement_Mega_Hit_Blaze_V3_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pitch-September-15-2026.md]]

- **Create the wallet** — "Create a personal e-wallet. **My money are kept safe in a vault server, which has been managed by JVL.**" Ticket denominations offered on screen: "You can have a **five dollar, ten dollar, or a hundred dollar**."
- **The PIN is a switch, and Val recommends off** — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-08-24-drop-pin-from-evoucher-flow.md]] (Sept 15).
- **Incremental cash-out, tied to the amount loaded** — "**If you put twenty dollars, you can cash out after four hands. But if you put a hundred dollars, you have to play at least ten dollars**"; Andrei's term for it: "**Incremental five.**" *(The arithmetic as spoken is not internally consistent and is left as it stands.)*
- **The ticket stays in the player's hand, which is the stated difference from TITO** — "**I still have this ticket in my hands because with TITO, you will put it here. And then you will have to reprint it. So you have two additional pieces of equipment needed just to operate**"; Val added the field cost: "**your technician is driving to replace paper or to remove a jam**" for a location "two hours away."
- **Move machine to machine on the same ticket** — "**Now I can move to another machine. Scan. Still keeping the same ticket**" — which is the direct answer to the customer's number-one complaint ([[wiki/organizations/unlimited-amusement.md|replay]]).
- **Lost ticket, recovered by the cashier from the cloud** — asked what happens to a $500 ticket lost on the way home, Andrei: "**Unfortunately, it's still as it is. But we have all the transactions… safely kept in the cloud**", and a location with a good relationship with a known player can have the cashier "**go into the interface, which only he can access**", agree verbally "**when it was and what the amount was**", and "**I can reprint. And you will get this ticket back.**" This is the customer-facing form of the reissue/reprint work on the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-14-transactional-mvp1-use-cases-and-ewallet-ticket-recovery.md|Sept-14 MVP-1 use-case session]] — **note it is presented here as available, with the caveat "it's still as it is" for the ordinary case.**
- **What the operator stops buying** — "**You don't need TITO printers. You save money and time on servicing them. Bill validators. You can still keep them.**" Cash top-ups survive: "Once you scan the wallet, you put money into the machine."
- **Two redemption paths** — cashier ("Cashier scans it. You have five hundred bucks… **Here's your card loaded. Here's your receipt**") or self-service ("**You go to this service machine. You scan it. You confirm it**… **Redeem, and it will dispense you a card**"). "**So you can redeem both ways.**"
- **Card portability is the operator's choice, not a product constraint** — "**So this is up to you to decide. Do you want money from this location to leave this location, or no?**"

**Three things the demo did not promise.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Unlimited_Amusement_Mega_Hit_Blaze_V3_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pitch-September-15-2026.md]]

- **Loading an existing third-party gift card back into a machine is InComm's call, not JVL's — and Andrei argued it is pointless.** Asked about a player holding "**10 or 15 InComm card**[s]… like a card, which is five thousand dollars worth five cards, but they don't have money, so how can they play?" — "**Technically, we can do that. It's up to InComm to make it worth it. But in the real world, as soon as a skill pay card is introduced, it will always end up in cash. So it doesn't make much sense for a technical solution.**" He did report that at the expo InComm "said that they're working on a solution that will make players be able to transfer credit from a debit card and a skill pay card game machine." See [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]].
- **The ATM leg is blocked by the clearing network, not by JVL.** Val, flagging it himself as the important moment: "**the clearance network prohibits in America. Prohibits proceeds from gambling. So the technology today exists with us and with everybody else. The legislative**[…]" — and "**It's connected to a network that will clear that card. Because it's not the machine; it's the clearing.**" What remains possible today: "**the only thing that realistically you can do is to redeem this card for cash at any ATM, depending on the ATM network**" — with per-ATM ceilings "**Some ATM have two hundred dollars. Some ATM have a thousand dollars**." And the standing plan: "**When Georgia Lottery will allow it, we will input. We will connect our dispensing unit towards it.**" *(One line renders ATM as "**ACM**" — "The ACM needs to be certified by an ACM support network" — and is **not** interpreted as anything other than the same garble.)*
- **Apple Pay / Google Pay sits with the integrator and costs more** — see [[wiki/topics/jvl-machine-pricing-strategy.md]] (Sept 15).

### The promo-credit-for-a-phone-number play, put to a customer for the first time

Val's clearest statement of *why* the transactional layer is worth owning, and the first time in the corpus the promo-credit mechanism is pitched as a **customer-acquisition instrument** rather than described as a capability. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Unlimited_Amusement_Mega_Hit_Blaze_V3_and_Transactional_Bundle_Pitch-September-15-2026.md]]

> "I present to you on this plate a choice: **would you like to print the ticket, or would you like me to send your booking in the form of a QR code to your phone? And because you give me your phone, I can give you five dollars of promotional credit.**"

- **The credit is playable-only and lands on the promo meter** — "**this puts it into promo meter**… So that promo meter now gets five dollars in promo credit. So **just play, no redeem**"; and on a $20 win plus $5 promo, "**the twenty dollars is cashable, the five dollars is gone.**" That matches the playable-only design already on this page (Aug 24, Pavel) and the SAS-2 promo capability Val described to InComm on Sept 2.
- **The point is the phone number, and then the marketing on top** — "**So I just showed you how you could get your customer phone number… when you got your customer phone number, we have a system where you could market to your customer.**" Worked example, volunteered by the operator ("when it's a slow day and nobody is there, we can tell our customer to come on Wednesday") and completed by Val: "**Come between three and four to this particular location… I will give you five dollars in promotional credit. We can scan this here. The customer comes in with the QR code on the screen, shows it, and automatically receives five dollars in promotional credit on the phone because we verified the phone number.**" *(Compare the Sept-2 version told to InComm, which used "between 3 p.m. and 4 p.m." and "$20". The **$5** here is the customer-facing number.)*
- **And the strategic argument the whole thing exists to serve — owning the player against the location.** "**Many times, your location is never your friend. They're never your partner… They are the people that extort money from you on a daily basis.**" On contract renewal: "The contract is over. **Three years have gone by, or five years**… They start negotiating… **Giving you cars. Giving you houses. Giving you a house in Florida**… **Your competition gives me this, this, and this. What are you willing**[…]" Then the counter: "**suddenly here you control the customer, you control the player**… **The moment I'm out of here, I'll start promoting that location over there. And all these customers that came to you here, now they all… will go there because they're not your customer; they're mine. So I just turned the tables for you**" — closing with "**we are the only system that will do it.**"
- *(Location revenue-share percentages cross the same passage as a broken series — "they will get a fifty percent, forty-seven percent, forty-three. Sorry, thirty-seven point five percent… Forty-three, forty-five. Forty-three," then "Forty-three point five percent." **Spoken loosely, self-corrected twice, and recorded as spoken; no single location-split figure is asserted from it.** Georgia's statutory split is handled on [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]].)*

### Two product claims made to the customer, recorded as claims

- **Everything is designed and made in house** — "**The hardware, the mechanical design, the industrial design, the hardware inside — all of the transactional system is designed by us in house. We do not buy anything outside. We manufacture. We design everything in house, from software to concept to complete implementation to manufacturing.**"
- **Other systems will probably be supported, and Val fenced it as unannounced** — "**we are probably this is not for sure we have not announced yet, but we'll probably support other systems as well**"; and on TITO specifically, "**It doesn't matter what it is. The technology.** The technology doesn't matter. **If you want TITO, we can offer TITO**", "**If you want to have bill acceptors inside of the machines, no problems. If you don't want it, we'll provide you with a system that will save you money. If you don't want to have a printer in your machine, you don't have to.**" The framing he gave it: "**we are business way agnostic. We're not telling you how to run your business. You tell us how you want to run your business. We will adjust ourselves.**" Andrei's version: "**We ask questions to understand your real need, and this need can be sliced per location, per audience, whatever**… **we can build the solution specifically for that location.**" *(This is the technology-agnostic position already on this page, stated to a buyer.)*

**§15.8:** clear — product, device and operator economics only; no named-person compensation. **§15.9:** no identifiers in the source.

## Sept 16 2026 — day three: half the requirements are blocked by their own open questions, and a location is allowed to keep trading with no internet

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-16-transactional-mvp1-open-questions-and-offline-operation.md|third consecutive 8:01 AM MVP 1 call]] (Russian, ~1h36m, same seven people). [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] had tried to start the non-functional requirements Val ordered the previous evening and could not, because "**половина требований имеют открытые вопросы, и как только Клода просишь с этим поработать, мы тут же получаем[:] у тебя там открытый вопрос**" — about half the MVP 1 requirements carry an unanswered open question, and the AI assistant will not work past one. The whole meeting went to her list, requirement by requirement: answer it, move it to MVP 2, or delete it [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]].

**The ruling that matters commercially: a location keeps working when the internet is down.** [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]] argued Flex IQ was specified never to run offline, because JVL must be able to block a terminal at any moment and an operator who can pull the cable and keep trading defeats that. [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] reversed it on business grounds — "**с точки зрения [про]дакт[-]о[у]нера, вы не можете позволить, чтобы лакейш[н] не работал**", and "**нас выгонят с лакейш[н]ов**" — and the settlement is: wallets are created locally, money is loaded, players move machine to machine and play; the **only** thing blocked offline is card issuance and redemption, which need InComm; the player's e-wallet stays in his hands; no internet at boot no longer prevents start-up while the lease is valid; and **lease expiry is the only thing that blocks**. A device may run **24 hours** without server contact — Val's working figure, which he himself said might be too long, against engineers who wanted no limit at all. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-a-location-keeps-working-without-internet-and-only-the-card-stops.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]].

**Val's second instrument: honesty about October 1.** Told that capability 116 assumes cloud behaviour that will not exist on the date, he said "**уберите это. Надо быть честн[ым] с самим собой… всё, что мы не можем сделать 1 октября, уберите**", and the rule was then applied down the list — requirements 1379, 1431, 1444, 1450, 1483 and 1485 all left MVP 1 in the same hour. The MVP 2 cloud capability promised on Sept 14 was confirmed created (on Sept 15), though the team could not identify its number with confidence. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-anything-that-depends-on-the-cloud-leaves-mvp1.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]].

**Numbers fixed on this call:** **15** Flex-Ports per location for MVP 1 with the system designed for **99** (Georgia in practice is one location, maximum 9 gaming machines) — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-fifteen-flex-ports-in-mvp1-and-the-system-designed-for-ninety-nine.md]]; **200 ms** between screens under load, **30 seconds** to wait for an activation answer, **30 seconds** for a player to collect at the Flex-Port — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-the-mvp1-timing-numbers-two-hundred-milliseconds-and-thirty-seconds.md]]. A request InComm never answers at all leaves a **pending transaction** until InComm says what happened [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]].

**Three product facts corrected from the floor**, all by engineers against the written requirement: there is **no cash on FlexPay in any form** — not via a recycler, not via a cashier — so the cash-return end state comes out of card issuance ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-there-is-no-cash-on-flexpay-in-any-form.md]]); a registered kiosk can be de-enrolled from a location but **never from the cloud database** ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-a-kiosk-is-de-enrolled-from-a-location-never-from-the-cloud-database.md]]); and **InComm returns no daily-cap error**, so JVL cannot detect one and the location's support call comes to JVL first, to be guessed at from the logs ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-incomm-returns-no-daily-cap-error-so-the-call-comes-to-jvl-first.md]]).

**And the cloud e-wallet got its first honest scoping.** [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] asked why the wallet is going to the cloud at all; the answers were central ticket validation, consolidation and bonuses (Val) and kiosk replacement at a location (Klimovich). Once Val confirmed there is **no** goal of cashing the same wallet at another kiosk, Biratka said the problem shrinks to "**нужна точка синхронизации**" — a synchronisation point. Val closed it as a **2027–2028 release**, not October 1 [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Open_Questions_and_Offline_Operation-September-16-2026.md]].

## Sept 16 2026 (10:12 AM) — the suite demonstrated to a master operator's CEO, and two unreleased FlexPort variants described out loud

Two hours after the MVP-1 call above, [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] walked the whole wallet flow in front of [[wiki/people/david-sloan.md|David Sloan]], CEO of [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md|ARC Gaming]], at JVL's Lawrenceville office ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-16-arc-gaming-cashless-suite-demo-and-class-a-cabinet-counter-offer.md|Sept-16 10:12 AM]]). **This is the first time the equipment is shown to ARC's chief executive rather than described to him.** [[raw/Meeting_Notes_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]]

### The wallet, end to end, in the demonstrator's own words

> "this **wallet is created in the server, it is protected**… and it's also **protected by pincode**… Now you can start the game, go to the game machine, **scan your pincode. Confirm the pincode. It shows your balance**… Now we can **move from one machine to another, still keeping it in your hands.** Every location might be equipped as well… either **cashier operated, kiosk pull there, or self service**… I can go to the same kiosk. Or… they can go to the **cashier. The cashier will scan with IQ and give the card to the player.** Working with this **Flexport**, you still keep your **building data per machine**… when they collect through the same system. **Just one ticket.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]]

*("building data per machine" is transcribed as spoken and **not** interpreted.)*

- **Wallet lifetime is a per-location setting:** "You can **set up a valid period for every single location. Normally it's thirty days**, so it becomes your wallet that you can keep with yourself" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].
- **The optionality line, stated twice in one breath:** "**The bill acceptor is optional. The bill acceptor and the printer are optional**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].
- **The Lego-bricks pitch, restated to a new customer:** "**You can build up a universal solution for all occasions, or you can build up one specific for each location**… you get a kiosk as a core factor like that, **a slim kiosk, and you can equip it with optical only and… TITO ticket for example, no bill validator, no card dispenser**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].
- **And the far end of the range — no paper at all:** "**We can absolutely do fully cashless, and we can actually eliminate tickets as well. We could transfer to your phone… and we can scan from the phone as well**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].

### Two FlexPort variants described to a customer before they exist

- **A surface-mount, fully enclosed FlexPort for machines with no cavity.** "**we don't have it now, but we're next. We're coming out with the same flex port, fully enclosed in a plastic, full plastic enclosure with two posts that could mount, that could be surface mount.** So instead of putting it inside the cavity. Surface mounted. **Some of your old machines don't have proper opening, so you just set it on post. You screw the post… and inside the post there is an opening for you to fish your wires through.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]]
- **"Version two, which will be Wi-Fi configurable."** Val's own framing, offered as the reason to look now rather than later — and the customer's reaction was to ask to see it: "**Yeah… all of it is Wi-Fi configurable. You install it. Install Wi-Fi… No, I like it. I said I'm not against trying it out**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].
- **Integration and install, stated plainly:** "**AFT integration. If you have second SAS port, you stick it into SAS port. If not, you stick it into the adapter**"; and units arrive pre-installed — "you get them already pre-installed… **The installation is simple, as five minutes**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].

### The two levers Val pushed hardest — neither of them hardware

- **Promo credits, and into the operator's own app.** "**Promo credit. So I could telephone promo credits to a player**" — and when Sloan said ARC has an app about to go live across all its locations and asked whether the credits could land there, "**Absolutely.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]]
- **Accounting with no people in it.** "**I can process accounting with no manpower. So I could send you a file of all of your accounting on a daily basis, and I can swipe your customers' ACH and bring it into your bank and reconcile with Microsoft… 365 Business Central**"; "**So I could send a single file.**" This is the Aug-31 pitch to ARC's COO, now made to the CEO [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].
- **And the honest frame around both:** "**we don't believe that there is any solution today in the market that is perfect, and the market is evolving**… **JVL is in unique position to provide full integration with the machine, the games, the transactional equipment**… **we are fast enough to react**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]].

**What the customer said back** — that his problem is the absence of a standard rather than the equipment, that he prefers cash because players chase it, and that his gift-card fix is being pulled out for lack of replayability — is on [[wiki/organizations/arc-gaming.md]] (Sept 16). **ARC's people were invited to come and see the kit on Friday Sept 18.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]]

## Sept 17 2026 — day four: a competitor product forces the design open, and the devices are told to defend themselves

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-transactional-mvp1-skillpay-multi-product-and-device-network-security.md|fourth consecutive morning call]] (Russian, 8:02–9:31, ~1h29m) — and the first of the four with **no Val in it**; five voices instead of seven [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]].

**The event that drove it happened outside JVL.** [[wiki/organizations/incom.md|InComm]] put **SkillPay** on the market on Sept 16 — a card that loads and spends like the existing $2 card but can be pushed into any certified ATM for cash. [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]'s read: players will strongly prefer it, so JVL must be able to issue it; and nobody knows yet how regulators respond, so JVL might build for a product that is closed a week later. **The answer to that risk was not to pick a card but to stop hard-coding cards at all** — the location record's single UPC field becomes a *collection of products*, each with its own UPC, fees and limits, held on the payment hub, with the UPC carried in the card's own barcode so the terminal knows on scan what it is holding. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]].

**What was NOT settled is the cashier's screen**, and it was parked on purpose — "давайте мы ещё поспим с этой идеей". Three positions stood: a product screen after the amount with no extra button ([[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]]); amount and product on one screen because the amount decides which products are possible ([[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]]); and keep it automatic with the maximum amount and the best product pre-selected ([[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]], arguing from a corporate fuel card you simply swipe). The pre-fill rule already written into the regulations — fill the redeemable balance, trim to the product's max cap, hang a banner — **silently chooses a product for the player once two max caps exist**, which is why it has to be reopened [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]].

**A product fact settled in passing: cents.** Pavel has already added cents to the Flex-Port — "**я на всякий случай в флекс[]порт сейчас добавил центы, потому что по [ACH] транзакции мы можем любую сумму туда[-]сюда загнать**" — because ACH can move any amount, so the transactions themselves support fractional sums even though a player can neither put a fraction of a dollar into a machine nor take one out. Klimovich backed it on portability grounds: "**если IQ потом куда[-]нибудь в Техас поедет, по идее, там всякая история может быть**". He also stated, hedged with "разве или ошибаюсь?", that Georgia redemption amounts come in multiples of **5**, and flagged that manual wallet creation will be limited to whole amounts — no wallet below **$1**, and possibly none below **$5**, which he left to think about [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]]. *(This whole exchange is absent from the derived notes.)*

**The second half of the call was device network security, and it produced the sharpest cost argument in the series.** Every JVL device looks straight at the internet through its own LTE modem with no router in front of it, so the operating system has to do the router's job — firewall, NAT, WAN/LAN profiles. The carrier cannot do it instead: JVL's cloud has an enormous dynamic address range and sits behind CloudFront, so the SIMs must be allowed to see the whole internet. What closed it was money, not threat modelling — an unfirewalled device gets pinged endlessly by bots, and JVL pays per megabyte on those SIMs. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-the-device-does-the-routers-job-firewall-nat-and-wan-lan-profiles-stay-in-mvp1.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]].

**The rest of the network list was split item by item** ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-bosiks-network-list-splits-between-mvp1-and-mvp2.md]]). The commercially significant one: **remote FlexPort firmware update leaves MVP 1**, because [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] pointed out JVL already updates 3,000 machines off a USB stick and six Flex-Ports is not a problem — with Klimovich's correction that it is the machine owners' technicians who carry the stick, not JVL. **Flex IQ still updates itself in MVP 1.** That reverses half of [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-remote-firmware-update-is-in-mvp1-and-flex-iq-updates-itself-first.md]]. The condition attached is a logging one: MVP 1 must still show that a device is down and that somebody has to drive out. Signal quality got the same treatment — for MVP 1 it means **remote** visibility, periodic metrics into the logs, buffered offline and flushed on reconnect, with Grafana on top ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-signal-quality-in-mvp1-means-remote-visibility-not-a-screen.md]]); LTE stays the default route with the manual override intact ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-lte-is-the-default-route-and-the-manual-override-stays.md]]); and the back-office stops being called "the cloud" ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-the-operations-portal-not-the-cloud.md]]).

**InComm's condition for verifying JVL as a broker** was also stated for the first time and is on [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]] (Sept 17): screenshots and video of the full flow **per product**, and specifically proof that the card is activated while still sealed inside its sleeve [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]].

**Val in absentia, twice.** Klimovich cited "**вал говорил… условно к понедельнику нам надо ещё продумать всю вот эту вещь, связанную с оперированием устройств по удалёнке**" — adding remote device operation to the Monday Sept 21 gate — and Pavel cited Val's standing rule that every device must carry a watchdog and reboot itself from scratch if it hangs [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]].

## Sept 17 2026 (1:47 PM) — the first built FlexPay Slim is inspected, and the finish is the finding

On the afternoon of the same day as the MVP-1 call above, Val went through a built **FlexPay Slim** item by item with [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi Peari]] ([[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-flexpay-slim-lid-critique-and-flexport-landed-cost.md|Sept-17 1:47 PM call]], Russian, ~7 minutes). He offered to fetch a camera and show the bay — "**я могу взять камеру и показаться**" — and Genadi said it was unnecessary: "**Мне всё понятно абсолютно**". Nothing about the transactional architecture changes here; what changes is the **product's physical standard**, and the reason Val gives for caring is commercial: "**Мы начинаем установки всех этих вещей, это будет критики полно**" — the installations are starting and the units will be picked apart [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Pay_Slim_Lid_and_Wiring_Critique-September-17-2026.md]].

The rulings — the flat **metal mesh on standoffs** over the boards and loose wiring instead of a box or a plate, the **two damped legs near the hinge** replacing the taut string that currently stops the lid, the gas strut removing the need for travel stoppers, self-opening left to Genadi, and the front-side wiring to be rerouted round to the back — are on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-a-metal-mesh-on-standoffs-covers-the-flexpay-slim-service-bay-and-legs-replace-the-string.md]].

Two things worth holding at the device-family level [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Pay_Slim_Lid_and_Wiring_Critique-September-17-2026.md]]:

- **The service bay is a designed space, not an afterthought — and the two men disagree about what it is for.** Genadi's position is that it is a locked service compartment opened only for service, so tidy wiring suffices. Val's is that anything a technician can see must look finished, and that an open bay is a physical risk — "**чтото может упасть**". The mesh is what settles it. This is the same argument, in miniature, as the Sept-16 ruling that moved the street machine's electronics to eye level behind a cupboard-door monitor ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-street-machine-electronics-move-to-eye-level-and-the-monitor-opens-like-a-cupboard-door.md]]) — **inference, labelled**: no source connects them.
- **The lid has no grip by design**, which is why the opening mechanism matters at all: "**вы же специально сделали гладкую поверхность, чтоб не за что было дёрнуть**". A smooth lid that must be prised open is the reason Val leans toward one that rises a little by itself.

*(The cost of the mesh is given only as "**буквально копейки**" and the damped legs only as "**какието доллары**" — no part price, material, gauge or supplier is stated in the source and none is inferred. The board-cost half of the same call sits on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-import-duty-gets-its-own-tax-category-in-business-central.md]].)* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Flex_Pay_Slim_Lid_and_Wiring_Critique-September-17-2026.md]]

## Sept 18 2026 — day five: the release is cut to fit the date, and the scope freezes on Monday

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-18-transactional-mvp1-capability-cleanup-and-the-monday-deadline.md|fifth and last morning call before the gate]] (Russian, 8:03–9:11, ~1h8m). Six voices; [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] is back, [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] took part **in the meeting chat only** and never speaks, and [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]] drove the session with nearly twice the speaking turns of anyone else. [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] dropped off at about 56 minutes for another call, so most of the feature planning ran without the product owner in the room [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]].

**Four things happened to the product, and three of them make MVP 1 smaller.**

**1. Rolling back a build is gone, and roll-forward is now the stated policy.** [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]]'s argument is a database one and it generalises past this release: the schema moves with the code, so an older build meets a schema it does not match — "**Удаляем таблицу [в]олет, потому что мы переезжаем в облако**", then the cloud does not work, and going back brings the system up empty. The fix is to re-cut the wanted version as a *new* one. [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]] turned it into doctrine — "**надо придерживаться всегда форвард… нашли баг, выпустили следующую версию**" — and Val wrote in the chat to drop it. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-no-rollback-to-a-previous-build-and-the-team-rolls-forward-only.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]].

**2. MVP 1 runs on dual SAS only, and the pilot locations will be picked to suit.** The second connection route — emulating the bill acceptor and the counters, i.e. the MudaGoose environment — is deferred, because supporting both is "**почти икс 2 тестирования**". MVP 1 locations will be chosen "**полностью эквип[ированные] д[у]о[-]сас машинами**", and the deferral is paid for with test automation so the second environment can be covered quickly afterwards. **Klimovich called the underlying market question unresolved in the same breath.** See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-mvp1-supports-dual-sas-only-and-locations-are-chosen-to-match.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]].

**3. FlexPay leaves the first release almost entirely.** Capabilities 117, 79, 80 and 116 are FlexPay work sitting in MVP 1 by mistake and went to MVP 2 with Biratka; 156 stays as a port Biratka copies across once Chudinov has built it on Flex IQ. **What is left in MVP 1 is Flex IQ and the Flex-Port** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]].

**4. Multi-product support is cut back to one product — contested at the time, and CONFIRMED by owner ruling 2026-09-20.** Klimovich, "almost finally": "**2 продукта мы к [MVP] один не будем делать**", with a single-product fallback ("**Мы можем сделать поддержку [скилпей]**") because SkillPay already works end to end. Val's chat message, read aloud in the room, says "**178 — [маст би MVP] один**". **Nobody reconciled the two.** See the open contradiction on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]]. **→ SETTLED by owner ruling 2026-09-20: "Klimovich is correct" — capability 178 / two-product support is NOT in MVP 1; Val's own chat instruction is withdrawn. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]].**

**Two product details worth keeping at the device-family level** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]]:

- **The balance display is game-machine agnostic, and the bar is the release, not the demo.** Capability 150 requires the balance to be shown on **any** machine — "**это [гейм] агностик[:] на любом гей[ме] нужно показывать баланс**" — in a form the requirements spell out. Klimovich says something exists "**в каком[-]то виде… но оно явно не очень**", and holds it to a human-readable release standard because real people read it off a screen. Pavel expects the gap to be design-only; **Irina closed off the obvious response — "мы не будем переделывать дизайн"**.
- **The receipt is one artifact with two printing logics.** The template is ready and the receipts themselves are identical on both sides — "**Да[,] одинаковые. Единственное[,] там логика печати этих ре[си]тов… чуть[-]чуть разная**". At the FlexPay the **cashier** chooses whether to print; in the self-service kiosk the **player** chooses whether he wants English — "**игрок выбирает, хочет английский или не хочет**". **What is still missing is the receipt format itself** — which fields print and in what order — and it is Klimovich's: "**конкретный ор[дер], что там печатать[,] это от Андрея**", who "**до сих пор не сказал**". That is the Sept-14 commitment below, still undischarged four days on.

**And nothing is being marked done.** Chudinov stopped the team closing finished capabilities, because the conflict sweep he ran over the whole MVP 1 requirement set has pushed new requirements onto capabilities whose existing features already closed the old ones — so a "done" feature may no longer cover its capability. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-nothing-is-closed-until-the-conflict-findings-are-worked-back-in.md]]. The full Jira-side sweep, every capability's owner and destination, is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-the-mvp1-capability-list-is-walked-item-by-item-and-every-capability-gets-an-owner.md]]; the read on the Monday gate is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-week-goes-to-non-functional-requirements-and-monday-september-21-is-the-deadline.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]].

**The freeze.** From Monday Sept 21 the MVP 1 scope closes and the team builds: "**больше ничего никуда долетать нельзя. Это в том числе сам себе говорю**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Capability_Cleanup_and_Monday_Deadline-September-18-2026.md]].

## Sept 17 2026 (3:17 PM) — a master licensee's requirements, and the admission that split redemption is not in MVP 1

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-17-dynamic-gaming-georgia-master-licensee-and-cashless-pitch.md|Dynamic Gaming meeting]] a Georgia master with about 130–150 locations told JVL what the product must do. His full list is on [[wiki/organizations/dynamic-gaming.md]]; JVL's answers are here. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dynamic_Gaming_Georgia_Master_Licensee_and_Cashless_Pitch-September-17-2026.md]]

### Counter first, self-service as an option

- **His ask:** redemption behind the counter, because "**the location owner likes to keep the control**", and one printer behind the register, not "**six printers or nine printers**" to refill.
- **Val's answer — start anywhere, redeem at the counter:** "**a player could start his play from a self checkout counter, from a kiosk… but we could force a redemption behind the counter. So a start could be self start, but redemption could be behind the counter.**"
- **The "Lego set" framing, from both JVL men:** Val: "**We're giving you a Lego set, as Andrei said, to do whatever business model you want**", and "**our AFTs built inside of the machines, which we call Flexport**" can do promo credit (his example: five dollars for scanning a QR code). The visitor's reply: "**first we fix the basic, then we add one step.**"
- **TITO stays optional.** Val: "**if an operator or master wants to operate the TITO program, it's okay. We can print TITO tickets.**"

### Split redemption — three destinations

- **The ask:** "**If I won five hundred dollars… hundred dollars back on machine number one… hundred dollars in lottery… hundred dollars in other gift cards**", with one generic button: "**You just need to have a button for other gift card.**" Val summed it up as "**three buttons**".
- **Andrei, in the room:** "**we are implementing your comments. You said that you need a lottery split… We are still in the stage**", and for the rest of a split balance JVL can "**print out the ticket and that is a redeemable ticket**".
- **Andrei, in the debrief:** "**I was actually lying to him a little bit about our priorities… because the split redemption is not possible with the one, but we can rearrange the order.**" And it is a common ask: "**my gut feeling tells me, like every second one was asking about, can I split the redemption amount into lottery tickets?**" (at the show). Felipe: "**it's not the first to ask for split.**"
- **Val's worry about the feature itself:** "**with the introduction of the InComm card, that whole split disappears**" — see [[wiki/organizations/incom.md]] (Sept 17, afternoon).
- *Earlier record:* on May 25 2026 a split was "architecturally possible but not built" for the July-5 target (above). On Sept 17 a customer names it as a condition for testing in his locations.

### The demo as shown, and the dates given

- **A prototype ticket flow was shown:** a PIN-secured wallet ("**there will be an option to do secured wallet or whatever you decide**"), partial collect ("**you have thirty-five dollars. Do you want twenty, ten, five, or all?**"), and a ticket that "**will work only at my store**" with the store's branding. Andrei: "**It's a prototype. We are constantly developing.**"
- **Dates, as Andrei gave them:** "**we were planning MVP1 to be available by the first of October. It's dual SAS and the next MVP2, which is end of October, is dual SAS plus single SAS on the same device supporting both.**" He called the roadmap "**not concrete**". The master said he has few single-SAS machines ("**I don't have a lot, but I'm asking**"). *(This fits the next day's ruling that MVP 1 is dual SAS only — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-mvp1-supports-dual-sas-only-and-locations-are-chosen-to-match.md]]; the "end of October" MVP 2 date is stated only here.)*
- **Brackets per model.** Andrei: "**every port comes already pre-installed into the bracket, and this bracket fits into a specific model**", so JVL wants the list of machine models before a pilot.

[[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dynamic_Gaming_Georgia_Master_Licensee_and_Cashless_Pitch-September-17-2026.md]]

## Sept 21 2026 — day six, the gate day: the screens are walked, and the freeze slips a day
