The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-21-transactional-mvp1-flexport-and-iq-ux-design.md|sixth morning call]] (Russian, 8:02–8:54, about 52 minutes), held on the Monday gate day. [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] has no speaker turn. [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] walked his finished Flex-Port and Flex IQ design; the Flex IQ screens are on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-flex-iq-cashier-flow-login-redemption-receipts-and-records.md]] (Sept-21 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**What changes and what does not.** Graphics stay; the change is the workflow and the texts. Pavel asked whether all the graphics change; Klimovich: "Графику мы оставляем нашу… скорее воркфлоу UX". Later: "Никакого нового визуального языка мы не предлагаем. Всё коллекция дизайна переиспользуется" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**The Flex-Port screen grid.** Every control fits a fixed matrix: "4 свимлейна горизонтальные есть 7. Колонок". The main change is a language button that lives in the top-left corner, which saves the player a step — he "можешь сразу сканировать тикет". A status zone on the right holds technical icons; details may wait for MVP 2 [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]]. **Screen size:** Pavel asked what resolution [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi]] gave; Klimovich: "854 на 480", with button sizes "почти один в один, как у нас было сделано с текущей версии" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**Status icons.** Klimovich wanted them only on the start-up and idle screens. Pavel: "где то будет 70% экрана это какието ошибочные экраны", where the icons tell the player something useful (for example, the machine-link icon blinking before a failed transfer). Klimovich agreed to show them on error screens too, but held that on player-facing screens a pictogram tells the player nothing: "Игроку нужна Информация на как ответ на его действия." Pavel: "с этим можно смириться" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**No paper in the printer — split by flow.** At the counter, no printer means no wallet; at the port, the wallet is created and goes to unclaimed, where the cashier prints it. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-21-at-the-counter-no-printer-no-wallet-at-the-port-the-wallet-goes-to-unclaimed.md]]. Printer tilts become a red indicator that opens a list, not banners over every screen [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**Which machine is a port on?** Klimovich raised it: IQ now works with ports, each fixed to one gaming machine, so every wallet and transaction needs a link to a machine. "в случае мутагуса это было всё просто" — the MudaGoose system returned "геймашин один гей машин 2" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

- **Today the port asks the machine over SAS for its number.** [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]]: "В нашем случае… это серийный номер машины. Что может быть у других? Мы узнаем только когда начнём получать эти данные." Pavel: the field defaults to the serial number but can be changed at installation, and other operators likely type "123… 124" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].
- **The asset number may be the better key — stated with doubt.** Pavel: "Похоже, что интролот вот этот вот параметр асад намбер он присваивает централизованно для всех машин", then "это я могу пофантазировать. Я не уверен в этом." [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] confirmed it is one per machine however many SAS ports ("Да, один"), and that it is set "если она работает с AFT и принтером" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].
- **What was agreed.** Chudinov extends the protocol to pass the asset number too; Klimovich asks the operators JVL is in touch with which identifier they use: "дима, ты добавишь тогда ещё и асид намбер окей, я уточню у операторов" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]]. This touches Val's Sept-15 worry about unit identification numbers ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-week-goes-to-non-functional-requirements-and-monday-september-21-is-the-deadline.md]]). *(Link is an inference.)*
- **Show it on the Flex-Port screen? Not now.** Pavel wanted the machine's ID on screen in place of the board's internal number. Klimovich refused: "конкуренты, пришедшие на локацию, увидев там айдишник машины могут, чтото с этим сделать? Я не знаю что." Pavel's alternative — a technician engineering menu opened with a QR code, showing asset number, serial and IP for about 10 seconds, then back to idle — went to **MVP 2**: "100% это MVP 2" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**A per-machine report leaves MVP 1.** Competitors' reports rest on detailed per-machine performance data plus maker and model; Klimovich moved it to MVP 2, Pavel agreed ("отдельный проект"). See the Sept-21 update on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-mvp1-stays-local-and-the-cloud-balance-requirement-moves-to-mvp2.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**The cloud leaves once more.** Irina created capability 229 for the cloud material, and Klimovich stripped payment-hub wording from four requirements under capability 158 during the call. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-anything-that-depends-on-the-cloud-leaves-mvp1.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**Multi-product.** Asked again, Klimovich said it was not settled and had to be decided that day; single-product fallback, likely SkillPay "если он останется на на рынке". **The owner ruling of 2026-09-20 stands** — two-product support is not in MVP 1; see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**Who builds what.** Port screens go to Pavel's side; IQ screens to Chudinov, who takes the main flow because it ties to the business logic, with [[wiki/people/vladimir-samusenko.md|Vladimir]] on the smaller pieces. Klimovich plans one container capability for IQ and one for the port, describing UI/UX briefly, with the rest carried by the HTML mock-up [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**The freeze slips.** "Торжественного фича лока и не будет сейчас" — maybe tomorrow, once his capabilities and requirements are in Jira. The team said it has enough work meanwhile. The gate read is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-the-week-goes-to-non-functional-requirements-and-monday-september-21-is-the-deadline.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_and_IQ_UX_Design-September-21-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** asset and serial numbers are discussed as fields only; no value is spoken.

## Sept 22 2026 (4:48 AM stand-up) — the Flex-Port screens arrive as an HTML page, and the redraw is split in two

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-22-daily-standup-capability-test-status-and-flexport-gui.md|Sept-22 Minsk stand-up]] (Russian), [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]] handed [[wiki/people/vladimir-samusenko.md|Vladimir Samusenko]] the job of redrawing the Flex-Port screens to [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]'s Sept-21 design [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**What arrived.** Klimovich shared the screens the day before as an interactive HTML page. Pavel: the workflow matches the current Flex-Port screens "Процентов на 95"; "положение кнопок, шрифты и прочее" do not [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**Two stages.** Pavel's plan: first "просто подвигать компоненты, которые у нас сейчас есть в соответствии с его схемой"; second, a full rework "с учётом… Цветов форму кнопочек". He expects Claude to handle stage one easily: "что сделает клод именно по перемещению компонентов это, скорее всего, легко пойдёт" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**Vladimir's objection — about the future, not this job.** Claude is not yet stable here: "иногда делает хорошо иногда делать… Отвратительно". And design should not arrive as an HTML page: there is an earlier design with "Дизайн токены" and component libraries "которые мы можем. Мы должны использовать", while an HTML page "потеряется её потом никто никогда не найдёт". [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]]: "мы не можем сейчас Андрею сказать… переделывай всё в фильме" (Figma). Vladimir: he will do the work — "я говорю вообще в будущем" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**Why HTML may not be the problem for this device.** Pavel agreed for screens that really render as HTML — "например, для флекса IQ или флекспея". The Flex-Port is different: its screens are built in a separate tool, "У нее тут своя разметка… это совсем не at TML". So converting from HTML to code may even be easier than from Figma to code — "Мне кажется даже вот так". He added that the Figma once given to outside developers ("сторонним этим евгениям") and what is drawn now "это совсем 2 разные вещи" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**The tool: TouchGFX Designer.** Vladimir is to install it, pull the Flex-Port project, import it and see what can be worked with. Pavel will send the installer, "потому что он недоступен на сайте с т м ой"; Klimovich had fetched it "както там хитро через клод". Claude moves components well once it has worked with the tool, but the screens must be imported back and recompiled, because the library that goes into the machine is compiled by TouchGFX Designer: "не не всё, клод может сделать на самом деле" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**The task waits for Klimovich's capability.** Pavel will create the conversion task for Vladimir, perhaps after the meeting with Klimovich, who "должен был… привязать туда этот свой график. Юзер интерфейс"; Pavel saw no new capabilities linked yet [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**MVP 1 remarks from the same call.**
- [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]], on extra metrics work: "мы не успеем все до 1 числа" — and time is very short "если сейчас захотят апдейты на флекспорты". Pavel: it was already decided that Flex-Port updates go to MVP 2 ("Ой на версию MVP 2"). Chudinov: "каждый день мы получаем новый список". The decision is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-remote-firmware-update-is-in-mvp1-and-flex-iq-updates-itself-first.md]] (Sept-22 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].
- **Wi-Fi is not MVP 1.** Pavel: there is only a basic connection; "мы это ж не делали вайфай и это точно не VP один". [[wiki/people/sergey-tsymanov.md|Sergey Tsymanov]] asked where the word MVP even comes from — "тут уже всё вот готовый продукт" — and got no answer [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].
- **A build may break.** Chudinov expects "развалится сборка пэ и этого рисалера" (unclear), with each product's overlay moved separately; Pavel: [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] can do it for FlexPay [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Capability_Test_Status_and_FlexPort_GUI-September-22-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** none.

## Sept 22 2026 (8:01 AM) — day seven: Val is back, the Jira list is cleaned once more, the October 2 end date is challenged, and the first end-to-end test is set for the next day

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-22-transactional-mvp1-jira-cleanup-and-e2e-tests.md|seventh morning call]] (Russian, 8:01–9:23, about 82 minutes), three hours after the stand-up above. [[wiki/people/val-levitan.md|Val]] chaired it; he said he had missed two calls. Seven voices [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**The screens: nearly done, and Val defers to the product owner.** [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] had finished the design logic for the IQ screen, laid out as he did for the port, and needed "3–4" more hours to finish and load it into Jira for both. On the Redeem screen he is adding a product choice; while there is only one product the field is "только информационное… невыбираемое". Val said he had not gone deep into the screens and that Klimovich, who sat in on every customer presentation, knows best what the screen must show: "Легко воспринимаю Андрея требования как догма сейчас". Klimovich's condition: the next IQ version must follow his UX and workflow, but "Я предлагаю переиспользовать текущий дизайн язык… Я не предлагаю ничего с нуля отрисовывать" — which matches his Sept-21 statement above [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**Two wording rulings from Val.** The fee line should not read "$1,000 max, $2": "тысячу долларов максимум и игрок получит на $2 меньше". Klimovich accepted and will show the product as "прайс пэй минимум $5 максимум 1000 фи $2", using the freed space for errors. And "эмпти" is the wrong English word; the right one, in the transcript, is "блэк" — read as "blank" (**inference**; the derived notes say "blank") [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]]. *(The $5 / $1,000 / $2 figures match the card values already on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]].)*

**The texts: one file, compiled at build.** Klimovich wants an English–Spanish text collection anyone responsible can edit, picked up by the next client build. Val named [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Andrey Bosik]] its owner and tied it to the standards (see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-point-claude-at-the-standards-repository-first.md]], Sept-22 update). Pavel posted the path where the Flex-Port translations already live — an XML file, English then Spanish per entry, with IDs — "Импортируется в софт… Прямо при сборке". Irina: that path covers the Flex-Port, not Flex IQ. Klimovich will convert his library to that format; a fix goes in by pull request and reaches the machine at the next build. Chudinov raised changing texts on the fly from the server; Klimovich would welcome it later but "не требую, чтобы мы к этому пришли [в MV]P один или даже 2" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**Val on who decides how.** When Klimovich drifted into how configs should reach the gateway, Val stopped him: "Вы путаете архитектурное решение" with a business requirement; if the best engineering answer is a cloud server, "в этом должно быть архитектурное решение, но не должно исходить от продакт[-о]унера". Klimovich: as product owner he may say how he would like it to work, and engineers decide how. Val: "Не как бы вам хотелось бы, а как маркет требует", citing Steve Jobs on not running market surveys. Both called it semantics [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**The Jira pass.** Capability 115 (FlexPay registration) left MVP 1 on Irina's argument that FlexPay is not checked or released in MVP 1 — Val: "Я согласен с Ириной". Capability 157 was emptied and moved to MVP 2; capability 180 takes the Flex-Port design and goes to To Do; 148 is the wallet-issue capability on Flex IQ; FIQ-34 got a date. Detail on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-the-mvp1-capability-list-is-walked-item-by-item-and-every-capability-gets-an-owner.md]] (Sept-22 update). Pavel still saw **capability 178** in Proposed as "единственное… из MVP один"; the owner ruling of 2026-09-20 that two-product support is not in MVP 1 is not reopened — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-jvl-supports-multiple-incomm-products-not-a-fixed-pair.md]] (Sept-22 update). Chudinov reported capability **122**, Flex IQ remote updates, closed that day [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**The kiosk is FlexPay.** Irina proposed splitting the initiative in two so the schedule shows MVP 1 alone — one for Flex IQ, one for FlexPay, which is MVP 2. Val: "мне нравится", and Pavel created it on the call. Val on the name: "я не люблю описывать киоск как киоск я бы хотел бы сказать что это флекс пей. Нам нужно быть [консистентны] к нашим стандартам" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**The date, challenged and left open.** Pavel's schedule shows MVP 1 work ending "до 2 октября грубо". Val: most is done, so "почему нужно ещё 10 дней?" Pavel: "Потому что до этого было 2 года." Chudinov: clarifying requirements must still be met, plus new functionality such as Flex-Port blocking. Val asked twice for a more honest answer, accepted that nobody wants to delay, and said: "вопрос оставлю открытым, хотел бы видеть другие сроки". See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-11-mvp1-goes-with-flex-iq-alone-and-one-kiosk-per-location.md]] (Sept-22 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**No end-to-end tests, and a one-hour test by tomorrow.** Most of the call went here. The ruling — autotests go inside the existing capabilities, and one connection test on the Flex-Port is written and run by the next day — is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-22-autotests-go-inside-the-existing-capabilities-and-the-first-e2e-test-is-a-one-hour-micro-step.md]]; the argument is on [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 22, 8:01 AM). Val's business case for testing JVL's own kit first: a Texas customer who wants "2 машины с нашим диспенсером… всё наше вся экосистема наша" — with his own caveat "у нас ещё нету диспенсера" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**IQ must run on the KORE SIM.** Klimovich recalled that the IQ and the game machine use different modems, and the KORE SIM has only been brought up on the game machine, yet "IQ [в] MVP один должен уже… работать с [LTE]… [К]оровским". Pavel asked him to fit the SIM in the IQ and leave it on for remote work; Klimovich will do it that day. The Georgia-office side is on [[wiki/projects/georgia-market-rollout.md]] (Sept 22, 8:01 AM) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Jira_Cleanup_and_E2E_Tests-September-22-2026.md]].

**Also on the call:** how Claude turns a capability into features ([[wiki/topics/ai-assisted-development.md]], Sept 22), and why dependency updates stay manual ([[wiki/topics/github-release-management-pipeline.md]], Sept 22).

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** none.

## Sept 22 2026 (1:01 PM) — AFT named to an ATM network as JVL's route to the player

On the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-22-ncr-atleos-readycode-cardless-atm-payout.md|Sept-22 NCR Atleos call]], asked whether JVL has any link to the player today, Val said: "At this moment, as we speak, we don't." Then he named AFT [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NCR_Atleos_ReadyCode_Cardless_ATM_Payout_Architecture-September-22-2026.md]].

- **The AFT insert.** "we offer an insert inside of a machine that we, in the industry, called AFT, Automated Fund Transfer." It "connects to machine counters" — "cash counter… promo counter… probably a list of thirty some counters inside of every machine" — which are now virtual, not mechanical. "through that AFT device, we can build various programs, digital programs that connects us to a customer. That's our way to the customer." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NCR_Atleos_ReadyCode_Cardless_ATM_Payout_Architecture-September-22-2026.md]]
- **A JVL app.** "There will be a JVL app, and there will be a touch point with the client, direct with the client." But JVL need not build it alone: "We could ride on your app and just put a layer of our own thing in it." NCR Atleos has no app. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NCR_Atleos_ReadyCode_Cardless_ATM_Payout_Architecture-September-22-2026.md]]
- **The player today** "is always a cash player", has no card, and is "reluctant to give us their phone number", though Val expects the business to "move into the direction of give me your phone number". [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_NCR_Atleos_ReadyCode_Cardless_ATM_Payout_Architecture-September-22-2026.md]]

## Sept 23 2026 (8:00 AM) — day eight: the UI/UX package lands in Jira, the open requirements are settled live, and the first tests come back half manual

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-23-transactional-mvp1-requirements-cleanup-and-autotests.md|eighth morning call]] (Russian, 8:00–9:18). Nine voices, Val present [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]].

**The screens are in Jira.** [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] loaded the UI/UX work that morning as two capabilities, **234** (Flex IQ) and **235** (Flex-Port), each with mock-ups, a text library and requirements that describe the screens — "Там прям всё прописано". He is responsible for keeping them current and telling the team of each change, so developers can have Claude re-check the features. His own requirements are all in Accepted or Published. Left for him in MVP 1: the IQ operator tilts and alerts as a separate text collection with codes; whether a confirmation screen is needed when creating or loading a wallet ("минут там 10"); home-screen icons in the kiosks' design language, drawn with AI; and a pass over Irina's NFR list for conflicts with product logic [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]]. Owners and the move of 180's screen feature are on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-18-the-mvp1-capability-list-is-walked-item-by-item-and-every-capability-gets-an-owner.md]] (Sept-23 8:00 AM update).

**The requirement pass.** About 12–13 requirements with open questions were settled one by one — REQ-753 deleted; no fixed disconnect timeouts, show the fault at once and keep retrying; no PIN lockout on Flex IQ; CAP-173 back to In Progress; REQ-1550 kept in MVP 1 with an ADR; requirements to be written short, in plain ESL English. Klimovich's rule: decide on the spot, park only what needs more than a minute. All on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-23-open-requirements-are-settled-on-the-call-and-only-slow-ones-are-parked.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]].

**Nine Flex-Ports in Georgia.** On REQ-1413, [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]]: "в джорджии 9 машин соответственно, у нас может быть максимум 9 флекс портов, но мы там ставили сейчас дефолт с запасом 20" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]]. Against the Sept-16 ruling (15 for MVP 1, design for 99) see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-fifteen-flex-ports-in-mvp1-and-the-system-designed-for-ninety-nine.md]] (Sept-23 update).

**No cloud view of the Flex-Ports in MVP 1.** Chudinov: the Flex-Port sends only logs and transactions, to Grafana; a view of which Flex-Ports are connected and what problems they have "в MVP один не будет" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]]. This fits [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-16-anything-that-depends-on-the-cloud-leaves-mvp1.md]] (**inference**; nobody named that ruling).

**Lost money becomes a "forfeit" wallet.** Five open Flex-Port bugs lose a player's money; Klimovich ruled the wallet is still created, with a separate status — money that exists but cannot yet be used. The bugs go to capability 231 [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]].

**FlexPay out of two more capabilities.** [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]]: two capabilities that held both FlexPay and Flex IQ were split that morning, the FlexPay part moved to MVP 2 ([[wiki/decisions/2026-09-23-capability-118-is-split-by-product-and-two-devices-keep-two-repositories.md]], Sept-23 8:00 AM update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]].

**The tests.** Eight connection tests, four manual; Pavel's symlink-to-/dev/null method makes them automatic. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-22-autotests-go-inside-the-existing-capabilities-and-the-first-e2e-test-is-a-one-hour-micro-step.md]] (Sept-23 update) and [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 23, 8:00 AM) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]].

**Server side after MVP 1.** On capabilities 230, 231 and 234, Pavel: the Flex IQ side will be ready, "но серверная часть это, наверное, уже будет после… м в п один" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_Requirements_Cleanup_and_Autotests-September-23-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** two passages withheld — see the meeting stub. **§15 #9:** none.

## Sept 23 2026 (11:05 AM) — the Flex Slim gets a lid, a Texas version, and ten sets; two IQ fixes

From the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-23-texas-flex-slim-release-street-cabinet-and-bom-source-of-truth.md|Sept-23 Val–Genadi alignment call]] (Russian) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]].

**Finishing the Flex Slim.** Val asked for the harnesses that join the boards to be made shorter so they do not lie "как бандал", and for the boards to be closed with a lid or a mesh: "обязательно закрыть это не это не годится так." Genadi said he had prepared a provision for it and "пока ничего не заказывали", and that a lid can be done. This follows the Sept-17 mesh ruling — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-17-a-metal-mesh-on-standoffs-covers-the-flexpay-slim-service-bay-and-legs-replace-the-string.md]] (Sept-23 update). On the monitor, Genadi said it had been assembled wrongly; he made a video and "прошёлся по всем пунктам", and expects a clean production result. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

**The Texas version.** Genadi checked it: "Интер кабинет никаких изменений не будет." The difference between the Georgia and Texas product is that the **card dispenser comes out** and a **recycler goes in where the bill acceptor was** (transcript "вместо билаксептера. Устанавливаем GCM"; "GCM" read as JCM — inference; see [[wiki/organizations/jcm.md]]). The existing mounts are reused; only **three extra parts** are needed ("Этот кейт дор"). He said he would release it that day, with a Texas bill of materials: "я его сегодня релиз и сделаю Белов материал на Техас". *(This fits the Sept-2 priority to take the recycler to Texas — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-priorities-iq-ports-flex-pay-recycler-texas-on-wallets.md]]; inference.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

**How many.** Genadi: "у нас есть 10 цетов ну этих прототипов", split between Georgia and Texas; the Georgia bill of materials is ready. Val had thought there were about 20; with 10, "нам на 2 месяца хватит." He asked for the three extra parts to be ordered at once, quantity 10. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

**Air or sea — left open.** Val: "10 и отправим их самолётом." Genadi: "А зачем нам самолёт?" — something (transcript "леса"; not clear what) will not be ready for a month and a half anyway. Genadi also pointed to the wait until something reaches Minsk ("пока ну Минск доедет"). Val: "в Минск он уже уехал. Я надеюсь что он самолётом он федоролэкспресс уехал" — it has already left for Minsk, he hopes by air (FedEx Express); he expects it there in about two weeks and thinks he can push it through in a week. His point was that the whole wait would be about a month, which a ship would not beat ("за месяц это не придётся кораблю"). What the shipment is, is not said. He then offered to order 5 by air. Genadi said small parcel shipments of bulk parts "просто выбрасывают деньги", and Val agreed: "Да, Я согласен." No final mode was restated. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

**The metal is the long lead.** Genadi: 50 sets of metal take "2 месяца"; if Val later wants deployment faster than two months, "у нас будет. Проблема." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

**Purchase orders and prepayment.** Genadi said he did not see Mikhail moving on the order and asked whether Val had told him to wait. Val: no — Mikhail had issued many purchase orders, which Val signed, but Val is managing cash. He wants the suppliers' terms negotiated so that either the release or the payment moves: "там везде стоит предоплата", which he called not reasonable. He planned to sit with Mikhail that day and go through every order to decide what to pay. *(That the 1:02 PM [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-23-open-purchase-order-payment-walkthrough.md|purchase-order walkthrough]] is that meeting is an inference.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

**IQ printer cover.** The mold will take a long time, so Genadi proposed rapid-prototyping a few covers. Val: "Ну 20 я бы заказал бы." Genadi: it is only a cover, it carries nothing, and nylon "не рассыпается". [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

**IQ mainboard fault — found and fixed.** The IQ mainboard did not work when switched on. There are two power connectors (an option for different, cheaper power supplies) and a sense line; "если не не воткнуто то оно закрачивает плюс на минус." Val: "его можно, ножиком просто ката трейс". Genadi had already given the team that workaround, and the board design is corrected. Val said he had not known about the problem. *(The transcript names the engineer who made the error; the attribution is withheld under §15.8.)* [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

*The derived notes give the same account, with the differences listed on the meeting stub* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]].

## Open contradictions

- **How much of the machine market lacks a second SAS port.** On Aug 24 2026 Val put the excluded segment at "гдето 15 20% рынка… гдето 10 15000 машин сегодня" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_Port_Mini_Capabilities_Design-August-24-2026.md]]; on Sept 2 2026 Pavel described the same excluded segment as "10% рынка где нету 2 сасса," with Andrei agreeing [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]. Both are speaker estimates about the same present-day market, made nine days apart, and no reconciliation was attempted on either call. Recorded; not resolved. **A third datum, the same day (Sept 2 2026, at the InComm lunch):** Val himself told InComm "**majority of the market today, 90% of the market has two SAS ports**" — i.e. 10% excluded, the figure Pavel gave that morning and not the 15–20% Val gave on Aug 24 [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_InComm_Machine_Payments_and_ATM_Recycler_Standoff-September-2-2026.md]]. He does not mention the earlier figure or acknowledge changing it, and no source reconciles them. **Recorded as evidence on the 10% side; the contradiction stays open.** **Two further data points, Sept 8 2026, at the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-08-dixie-amusement-bundle-offer-and-card-economics.md|Dixie visit]], and they pull further apart, not together:** Val put the single-SAS installed base at "**about ten thousand machines**" of "**forty-seven thousand machines in the market**" — roughly **21 %**, which matches neither his own Aug-24 15–20 % nor his own Sept-2 10 %; and [[wiki/people/salim-khambawala.md|Salim Khambawala]], a master licensee speaking from his own route, said "**we are seven percent ahead, which is dual [SAS], and rest is still that single [SAS] market**" — i.e. roughly **93 % single-SAS**, the exact inverse of Val's Sept-2 statement to InComm. Neither man challenged the other's figure and the two numbers were spoken minutes apart in the same room. **Four estimates now; the contradiction stays open and widens.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dixie_Amusement_Georgia_Transactional_Equipment_and_Bundle_Economics-September-8-2026.md]] **A fifth datum, Sept 16 2026, from Val to ARC's CEO:** opening the demo on the bill-acceptor-input route into a machine, he said it "**will address probably 10,000 machines and locations that are single**" *(the sentence is clipped; "single" reads as single-SAS from context — an **inference**)*. That is the same order as his own Sept-2 and Sept-8 ten-thousand figures and not his Aug-24 one. **Recorded as further evidence on the ten-thousand side; nothing here reconciles the estimates and the contradiction stays open.** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Gaming_Cashless_Suite_Demo_and_Class_A_Cabinet_Counter_Offer-September-16-2026.md]] **Two earlier data points, back-filled (Aug 25 and Aug 27 2026):** at the Mutha Goose maker's shop on Aug 25 the maker said "The old stuff's all single" and that at a ten-machine gas station "there'll be two out of ten" with dual SAS (~80 % single), while Val in the same passage guessed "maybe ten thousand" single-SAS machines [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Epic_Amusement_Kenny_Patel_Visit-August-25-2026.md]]; and on Aug 27 Val told ARC "**probably twenty percent of the market**… still has single SaaS port" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_ARC_Coffee_Fleet_Swap_and_Transactional_Pitch-August-27-2026.md]]. Seven estimates now; still open.

- [ ] Genadi Peari — release the Texas Flex Slim configuration (card dispenser out, recycler in place of the bill acceptor) and its bill of materials — due Sept 23 2026 — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]
- [ ] Genadi Peari — order the three extra parts for the recycler, quantity 10 (shipping mode unresolved) — due unspecified — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Texas_Flex_Slim_Release_Street_Cabinet_and_BOM_Source_of_Truth-September-23-2026.md]]

## Open questions

- How far should the floating FlexPort stand off the machine's front panel — one inch or two, if the TV overhangs? — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Layout_and_FlexPort_Enclosure-September-24-2026.md]]
- **Can split redemption (machine / lottery / "other gift card") be moved ahead of the Oct-12 demo?** Andrei said it is not in MVP 1 "but we can rearrange the order"; no one committed to it — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Dynamic_Gaming_Georgia_Master_Licensee_and_Cashless_Pitch-September-17-2026.md]]

- Whether to keep installing at some locations on **MudaGoose** in the interim, now that JVL is moving away from it. Raised **twice on the same Monday**: at 8:00 Klimovich asked whether JVL should go in on MudaGoose "хотя бы там пару месяцев" during the pilot and Val answered "Не знаю… мне кажется, что это мы только [со]здадим какую-то путаницу"; at 10:02 Chudinov raised it again, Klimovich put it to Val, and Val said "не думаю" but asked for a day or two. **Unresolved.** — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]]
- Whether **two gateway devices on one location** — a Flex-IQ and a self-service kiosk together — can work with each other. Klimovich put "большую звёздочку" against it and parked it: "давайте пока[,] наверное, одна локация[ —] один гейтвей… для простоты обсуждения, а потом можем усложнить всю эту систему." (Sept-7, 8:00.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_GACS_Expo_IQ_FlexPorts_and_Ewallet-September-7-2026.md]]
- Whether **receipt printing after card activation** is in the current Flex-IQ build. Klimovich asked; nobody answered. (Sept-7.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Notes_SAS_AFT_on_FlexPort_and_Remote_Update-September-7-2026.md]]
- Should the Flex-IQ scanner be angled slightly forward rather than straight down, so items wider than a card (Pavel's example: a ticket printed by a third-party machine) can be scanned? Andrei deferred it and questioned whether redeeming other vendors' tickets is even a business requirement — "Мы обсуждаем только полностью [closed-loop] системы". (Raised by Pavel, Sept-2.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- Should an e-wallet stay scoped to the location that created it? Currently yes; Andrei sees no market demand to change it, Val is not sure that is the design JVL wants, and Pavel's server-side "friendly locations" flag is the proposed later mechanism. (Sept-2; detail on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-02-e-wallets-move-to-the-cloud.md]].) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- Which time zone governs the Dwolla ACH retry? The team agreed on **08:00** but could not agree whether that is Georgia's zone or, as Pavel put it, "Центральноамериканская". (Sept-2.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Flex_IQ_Barcode_Receipt_Ewallet_Strategy-September-2-2026.md]]
- Flex-Recycler **server topology** — an in-machine server (auto-expiring tokens, 24h reset) is sufficient per Val, but JVL is also building an external server-side component for operator reporting/analytics and recurring revenue; where one player's e-wallet transaction state lives across machines is unresolved (no formal ADR). — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_flex_recycler_req_daily_sync-April-29-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_flex_recycler_req_daily_sync-April-29-2026.md]]
- Flex-Recycler pricing model — **lease-with-deposit** (matching Vanilla/Pace) vs outright hardware sale; not decided. — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_flex_recycler_req_daily_sync-April-29-2026.md]]
- Where does the redemption device belong — behind the cage or self-service on the floor? Val: "it's to be decided"; he doubts fully self-serve ("If this were possible, then Amazon self-checkout stores would prosper everywhere"). — [[raw/flex_pay_evolution-april-8-2025.md]]
- ~~Wallet validity window: 30 days vs ~24 hours — needs master feedback.~~ — [[raw/flex_port_recap_felipe_juan_pavel-april-8-2025.md]] **Resolved Sept 14 2026:** a TTL set at wallet creation, 30 days by default, configurable per location between 1 and 365 days, with the validity period printed on the carrier. Val raised the "why hold an empty wallet for days rather than hours" version of the same question and accepted Pavel's answer that separate empty-ticket logic costs more than keeping it. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-ewallet-ttl-defaults-to-thirty-days-and-is-set-per-location.md]]. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]
- ~~Should the recovery of a carrier that failed to print be a **reissue** or a plain **reprint**?~~ **Resolved Sept 15 2026 as reprint.** Klimovich re-argued for reissue on universality grounds and lost to Chudinov's point that reissue protects against nothing a photograph can already defeat while complicating the transaction history; he had already switched the design. (Detail on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-the-ewallet-is-created-before-its-ticket-prints.md]].) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Balance_Naming_and_UI_Flow_Walkthrough-September-15-2026.md]]
- Should **capability 171 (location block enforce)** and **capability 118 (terminal lease and remote disable)** be merged? Pavel flagged them as the same thing; Klimovich agreed they overlap strongly but kept 171 to carry the per-device detail. Left as is, not decided. (Sept-14.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Use_Cases_and_eWallet_Ticket_Recovery-September-14-2026.md]]
- Can JVL's cage software run on a location's existing POS hardware? Complicated by mixed OSes and the player-PIN entry need — left open. — [[raw/evolution_flex_pay-april-11-2025.md]]
- How is a Prize Card's value communicated to a machine with a **single SAS port** vs one with **two SAS ports** (raised on the Aug-6 Prize-Card discovery)? — [[raw/Flex-Port-use_case-August-6-2025.md]]

Alan and team have not answered; JVL's "don't process above-limit at all" rule stays provisional until today's PAX cycle is understood. (Raised Jul-22.) — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MG_IQ_Escrow_Transaction_Flow_Complete-July-22-2026.md]]
- How to detect that an **IQ changed location** post-MVP — GPS won't work indoors and the external IP address is unreliable. (Raised by Andrei, Jul-30.) — [[raw/Meeting_Notes_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_IQ_Unit_Location_Binding-July-30-2026.md]]

Both were asked on Sept 17 and neither was answered; a production card has to be swiped and the logs read. — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]]
- How do the **firewall/NAT changes get tested** without breaking the device operating system? Pavel said outright he does not know; three ideas were floated on Sept 17 and none was adopted. — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_SkillPay_Multi_Product_and_Device_Network_Security-September-17-2026.md]]
- What is the FlexPay connection/service fee: "roughly three dollars a day… Roughly a thousand dollars a year" per terminal (Val, Feb 27 2026) or "thirty dollars a month for the… service", with the modem plan on top (Val, Mar 4 2026)? Five days apart; no source says the figure changed or reconciles them, and either could be a garble. — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rich_Winley_FlexPay_Rollout_and_Texas_Brokerage-February-27-2026.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Rich_Winley_Kiosk_Delay_and_Tribal_Casino_Trial-March-4-2026.md]]
- On the Aug-10 call, which operator would not sign, which certificate is needed from the "masters", and what does "four weeks until this reaches us" refer to? None is stated. — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Mutha_Goose_Flex_IQ_Printer_Detection-August-10-2026.md]]
- Is "fifty bucks a month… per location" a **Fiery Pay** price, and who is "he"? Val's guess on Aug 18 was queried and never confirmed. — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Mutha_Goose_Supplier_Dinner_Intel-August-18-2026.md]]

## Sept 24 2026 (8:01 AM) — day nine: the mock-up is final, two lost-money bugs get rules, unclaimed handling waits for MVP 2, and October 1 holds

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-24-transactional-mvp1-flexport-money-loss-bugs-and-release-date.md|ninth morning call]] (Russian, 8:01–9:11). Nine voices, Val present [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_Money_Loss_Bugs_and_Release_Date-September-24-2026.md]].

**The mock-up is final.** [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]: "Я считаю, что он в финале уже". He fixed the conflicts between the mock-up and the requirements, reviewed the non-functional collection against business and functional logic, and finalised the IQ tilt logic with a code for each error. The tilts stay in the one text library, not a separate document — two documents would mean editing both. He added icons to the home screen [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_Money_Loss_Bugs_and_Release_Date-September-24-2026.md]]. He had posted a Jira change log to a chat that Val and Pavel missed; Val ruled such records go by email — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-24-written-records-go-by-email-and-chats-are-for-instant-matters.md]].

**Two lost-money bugs, two simple rules.** Bug **349**: a Flex-Port that dies while holding a player's money keeps the transaction and, when it comes back and reaches a gateway, sends the money to unclaimed, with no dialog to the player. Bug **354**: a Flex-Port without its gateway link goes out of service, collect is refused, and the money stays on the machine. Klimovich: "Давайте простую какуюто логику… потом будем наблюдать в реальности" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_Money_Loss_Bugs_and_Release_Date-September-24-2026.md]]. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-24-a-flex-port-that-dies-holding-money-sends-it-to-unclaimed-and-one-without-a-gateway-goes-out-of-service.md]].

**Unclaimed money: keep it, do nothing with it yet.** Val asked why a working Flex-Port gives up the money after 30 seconds, and floated a PIN as with AFT cards. The answers: SAS lets money stay on the machine for about 8 seconds; money on the port belongs to no wallet; tying it to a player is a player account. Pavel's nearest-wallet match drew Val's "Это очень хорошая", but Klimovich would not give the cashier a way to move unclaimed money yet, and Val agreed: "Андрей прав, мы это сейчас не поднимем" — MVP 2 [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_Money_Loss_Bugs_and_Release_Date-September-24-2026.md]]. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-15-a-timeout-sends-the-money-to-unclaimed-and-the-player-never-loses-it.md]] (Sept-24 update).

**Cashier screens and service screens** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_Money_Loss_Bugs_and_Release_Date-September-24-2026.md]]:

- **An expired receipt choice becomes "no receipt".** Chudinov asked what happens when a cashier screen's idle timer runs out. Klimovich: the timer shows after 15 seconds, and after 15 more "мы воспринимаем действие, как ноурисит автоматом". Such transactions go to Records, where reprint is available. Receipts for pending transactions must also be reprintable; he will mark it.
- **Out-of-service screens name the cause.** Pavel: instead of a bare "machine" error they can say the machine door is open, the machine is locked, or the QR scanner does not work. Klimovich asked for the full list of tilts and errors, each with a recommended action where one makes sense.
- **A version footer on service screens.** Pavel: "Снизу будет добавляться версия и serger, потому что это важная информация для поиска проблемы" — "serger" read as serial number is an **inference**. Irina: players do not need it. Klimovich: it makes sense for initialisation, idle and service messages, and he will add it to the mock-up, since Chudinov uses Claude to check mock-ups against the built screens. [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]] asked that Flex IQ also show the build version only in service modes; Klimovich said that was the plan.
- **The status bar** with icons shows on every screen except where the zone is taken by a player action.

**Autotests, hooks and commits** — on [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 24, 8:01 AM) and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-main-branch-must-always-build-and-the-code-belongs-to-the-project.md]] (Sept-24 update).

**The date.** Irina: October 1 "будет 50 на 50" on test coverage. Val offered October 15 and linked it to Klimovich being away. Klimovich objected that 15 could become 30, and wants the build received, installed and tested in Georgia on October 1 even if half fails. Val: "давай согласен, это правильно." **Plan of record: October 1, unchanged** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_MVP1_FlexPort_Money_Loss_Bugs_and_Release_Date-September-24-2026.md]]. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-first-transactional-installation-planned-for-october-1.md]] (Sept-24 update).

**Notes vs transcript.** Where the derived notes differ (feature counts, the task number, "serial number"), the transcript is followed; see the meeting stub [[raw/Meeting_Notes_MVP1_FlexPort_Money_Loss_Bugs_and_Release_Date-September-24-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** one personal passage withheld — see the meeting stub. **§15 #9:** none.

## Sept 24 2026 (4:38 PM) — a FlexPort revision: fully enclosed, on posts, "floating" off the machine

Near the end of the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-24-hardware-repo-bom-source-of-truth-and-street-cabinet-layout.md|Sept-24 4:38 PM Val–Genadi alignment call]] (Russian), Val raised it as a separate topic, "пока я не забыл" — not for the street machine ("Это, конечно, не касается острить машины вообще"). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Layout_and_FlexPort_Enclosure-September-24-2026.md]]

- **What Val wants.** "Нам нужна ревизия флекспорта, который полностью закрыт. В пластиковой коробке", with fairly thick posts ("ножки довольно толстые") that fix to the machine, so the whole port stands off the machine by about an inch — "флори дизайн" (floating). The reason: "есть многие машин, которые… Этот катаут нестандартный." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Layout_and_FlexPort_Enclosure-September-24-2026.md]]
- **Where the posts fix.** "На передней панели на передней панели не сбоку." They are both the mounting and the wiring path: "Они являются и и крепежом и и проводкой", like conduit pipes. Val thinks the posts may come in different sizes. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Layout_and_FlexPort_Enclosure-September-24-2026.md]]
- **Genadi: it is nearly there.** Today's external plastic base, closed with a back cover, "уже сделан так что… всё что надо это калор сзади"; the tubes can be moulded into that same cover ("22 трубочки" / "те же 2"), and the machines' panels already have four holes the ports screw into from the other side. "Это просто, что надо сделать это одну… Одну крышечку." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Layout_and_FlexPort_Enclosure-September-24-2026.md]]
- **Stand-off distance not fixed.** Val: "я не знаю на какой дистанции это держать, потому что может быть телевизор свисает… Может быть надо не один и чато там 2 нча". Genadi: in the machines he has seen there is usually a lot of room. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Layout_and_FlexPort_Enclosure-September-24-2026.md]]

*(The derived notes place the four holes in the new cover; the transcript places them in the machines' panels. The wiki follows the transcript.)* [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Street_Machine_Cabinet_Layout_and_FlexPort_Enclosure-September-24-2026.md]]

## Sept 25 2026 — a hidden engineering menu for technicians, and the missing Flex-Port screens all have tasks

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-25-daily-standup-engineering-menu-and-jira-discipline.md|Sept-25 Minsk stand-up]] (Russian) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Engineering_Menu_and_Jira_Discipline-September-25-2026.md]]:

- **Missing screens are covered.** [[wiki/people/vladimir-samusenko.md|Vladimir Samusenko]] finished the Flex-Port fixes after Andrei updated the description. Screens he cannot build yet (no firmware) all have tasks: "Они все подкоп обилити 156" (under capability 156). The collector screens need Flex-Port tasks 14 and 15; the claim screens need tasks 63 and 64 — Pavel: a back end is needed first to bind them. His design task is "торт 52" (read as port 52); all commits go into the "порт 9" branch, so "Все эти задачи пойдут вместе." He will send Pavel the artifact that describes all this, for Pavel's Claude or to read. He expects to finish in "часа полутора" and then needs new work.
- **Claude builds from the mock-ups first.** [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]]: for now, let Claude look at Andrei's mock-ups and build the screens close to them; at the end, walk through and check the mismatches. (Andrei = [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]], **by context**.)
- **An engineering menu — Pavel's idea for the free time.** Pavel: sketch a page in TouchGFX ("в тач GFX какуюто страничку набросать") for "инженерного меню", showing critical information that is not available now. Vladimir: for the developer or the operator? Pavel: "Скорее для технического персонала", who reach it in a special way, "например, сканированием специального кюара". It may have several pages: a status screen (Wi-Fi connection, signal level), a second screen with the last transactions, and a screen of recent errors — the time, what failed, "пропадание связи, отваливание, какогото устройства, не прошедшие транзакции". Most critical for Pavel: the IP addresses received, because "в продакшн билл день не будут доступны", plus link state. Vladimir: "Чтонибудь накидаю, тогда потом обсудим."
- **This does not change the Sept-21 scope call.** On Sept 21 the QR-code engineering menu went to MVP 2 ("100% это MVP 2", see the Sept-21 section above). The Sept-25 ask is a sketch for discussion; no MVP is named. *(That it stays MVP 2 is an **inference**; nothing on Sept 25 moves it.)*
- **Later work.** Pavel named "линк кволите девайс стэйт даш борт пердевайс" — a per-device link-quality and device-state dashboard — as work for later ("Это потом да?").

## Sept 25 2026 (1:30 PM) — the promo flier: what the transactional family is called, and the four problems it solves

On a [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-25-promo-flier-review-and-sergey-vysotsky-wait.md|16-minute call]] Val read [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]'s draft JVL promo flier line by line (English, poor audio; then Russian). Only the clear points are kept [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]].

**How the flier describes the product.** Val read out: "One floor, one system, every state"; "a modular transactional platform for multi-state operators"; "Load play redeem all match from one view" (as transcribed); then the product lines — Flexport, "AFT, all capital letters", "With touchscreen", "An optical reader", Flex IQ, "Cashier POS", "Flex Play Slim", "Self-service. Payment kiosk", and an operators' "Dashboard" with "transactional and service information" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]].

**Val's changes (English part).**
- "Build to upgrade your floor and not replace it. **Remove it, please.**" Klimovich: "Okay." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]]
- The two section headers read "**The problem we address, the solution we offer**" — "no orange in one font in one size" (as transcribed) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]].
- The first problem is "**the cost of cash management**" — "per machine validated printer location by location" — tied to the Flex Recycler ("Cost of cash management at Flex Recycler") [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]].
- **Data ownership.** "Data ownership hints with data management… the ownership is not it is not negotiable." Val's proposed line: "**Your floor runs on your data. It should be yours to see, keep, and use.**" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]] *(This is the same theme as operators owning their machine data on [[wiki/topics/machine-data-repurchase-program.md]] — linking them is an inference.)*
- Val also said "Promo management, but most it's a flex part 2" — meaning **unclear** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]].
- A JVL logo / "JVLUS headquarters" / "building background" point was made; the exact change is **unclear** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]].

**The four problems (Russian part, Klimovich).** [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]]
1. Cash management.
2. Data management — "всё разобрано на разных… Файлы, где то все существует, не используется эффективно" (spread across files; it exists but is not used well).
3. He was unsure how to word it: "нету решения, которое бы позволило быстро эффективно настроить локацию или под селсервис или под тенден" — no solution to set up a location fast for self-service or for "тенден" (likely *attended*, staffed — an inference); "У нас один пакет, который предлагает это решение, хотите так хотите этак".
4. Promo management — "у вас нету аутрия к вашим плеерсу они вот приходят и уходят. Нету ретаргетинга." Val: "не знаете кто" (you don't know who they are).

Klimovich: "Вот 4 проблемы, мы согласны с этими, просто их надо 3 писать подругому" (the "3" may be garble). Val: "Да, наверно." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]]

**The solutions part is not reviewed yet.** Val read it for about a minute: "Мне тяжело на лету… когда все слова поменяются. Мне тогда легче будет". Klimovich: "давайте я пришлю вам обновлённую версию по этим комментариям" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Promo_Flier_and_Vysotski_Deployment-September-25-2026.md]]. Commitments on [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md]] (Sept 25, 1:30 PM).

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** none.

## Sept 26 2026 (9:36 AM) — what the PORT 9 branch holds, a Telnet console on the board, and the GUI harness

A 9-minute Saturday [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-26-flex-port-telnet-console-and-gui-harness.md|Flex-Port development sync]] (Russian; [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel Frolov]], [[wiki/people/evgeniy-alekseev.md|Evgeniy Alekseev]], [[wiki/people/alexei-khamin.md|Alexei Khamin]], [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina Shishko]]). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**What is in the branch.** Pavel: "в рамках вот порт 9… Добавлена поддержка. Сас, то есть перетянутая из Того проекта, который был с сасом"; "А FT команды непосредственно общение с асом"; and "какая то часть работ по вайфай модулю е с п 32 c 6". The next pull requests, "порт, 51 и порт 68", will be committed into it too. That is the SAS/AFT branch Pavel described on Sept 14 — see [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-flex-port-sas-mode-is-a-selection-capability-not-a-long-lived-branch.md]] (Sept-26 update) — and the Wi-Fi work on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-wi-fi-module-on-flex-port-with-an-external-router.md]] (Sept-26 update). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**A Telnet debug console on the board.** "Нам сейчас тилнет сервер загружен… можно к платье подключаться потелны, а то у нее там богатый набор команд" — for debugging: the SAS channel state, Wi-Fi, Ethernet, the IP address, and "включить режим сас или включите режим ноу сас". Typing help lists all commands. The same console works over the UART — "который трехконтактный" — but without pictures. [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**Screenshots and a web page.** On a separate port a service takes screenshots of what the Flex-Port shows; a web page shows the screen and emulates actions — "отсканировать тикет или там, например, вот прописать вайфай сеть". [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**How to run the GUI harness** (Alekseev wanted the page for himself). In the project's "тулс папочка": "Гуй харнес это питоновский скрипт". You give it the Flex-Port's IP address; "в последних прошивках этот IP адрес даже на экране будет высвечиваться". It starts a local web server, default port "80 73" (localhost). It has two channels: Telnet on port 23, and a second port "какой то 5000 чем то" that returns a raw picture, which the server turns into a PNG for the page. It refreshes on click, not all the time, because "там пол секунды идёт скачивание картинки". [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**Not ready: the firmware simulator** — "пока что на симулятор не готов для прошивок. И, наверное, сегодня завтра не будет готов." See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-virtual-test-system-tries-full-arm-emulation-first.md]] (Sept-26 update). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**Autotests** through the board's hooks — on [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 26). [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**New boards arrived.** Alekseev: "Платы пришли" — the count is garbled ("2 этот новых да 2 типа ещё 3 дороги") — and "поиспитываем" (we'll test them). Pavel had seen the photo. Pavel closed: keep working "В общем направлении". [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_FlexPort_Telnet_Console_and_GUI_Harness-September-26-2026.md]]

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** none.

## Sept 28 2026 (4:56 AM stand-up) — Syncotek first, the engineering menu under capability 235, and the latest Flex-Port on a real machine

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-28-daily-standup-log-traffic-and-nova-rename.md|Sept-28 Minsk stand-up]] (Russian) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_Log_Traffic_and_NOVA_Rename-September-28-2026.md]]:

- **Card dispenser: try Syncotek first.** [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] is bringing capability 134 (features 12 84, 12 85, 12 86) to full card-dispenser support "по аналогии с флекс айкью". Two dispenser types are supported; at start the software checks each and picks the one that answers. Pavel: "пусть пробует синка текст сначала" — it will be used in about "99%" of cases; the second type only if a location bought a few of those.
- **Bills to accept.** Biratka worked on capability 133 over the weekend — choosing which bills to accept. To close it he must feed bills into the kiosk and check. He expects 133, 134 and 137 (updates) to close soon; next is moving the GSM-modem control code to the Flex-Port. Pavel asked him to put start and end dates on his tasks.
- **The engineering menu gets a home.** [[wiki/people/vladimir-samusenko.md|Vladimir Samusenko]] is building the Flex-Port service menu and found no capability to link it to. Pavel first offered a new capability, then: add a separate requirement under the capability for screens and texts — "экраны для… [д]иагностики… инженерное меню" — and a new feature will come from it; "так даже проще". Vladimir named it: "235". This follows the Sept-25 sketch above; no MVP was named. *(That it stays MVP 2, as on Sept 21, is an **inference**.)*
- **The latest Flex-Port goes on a real machine.** Pavel: the recent fixes "проверялись только на эмуляторе", and at the start work done only on the emulator did not all work on the machine. [[wiki/people/ivan.md|Ivan]]: "Я к себе поставлю." Ports sit on two or three machines; Pavel has two more, "самые продвинутые у них вайфай есть".
- **Card activation.** [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]] finished card activation; his tests were finishing, and he would push capability 237 — the dashboard of activated cards — that day. Pavel: the overdue tasks can be closed "по пэймэнхабу".
- **Wallet on the Flex-Port.** [[wiki/people/sergey-tsymanov.md|Tsymanov]] finished wallet creation on Flex IQ and took the next task, wallet creation through the Flex-Port (the name is garbled; read from the 8:02 AM call — **inference**).

## Sept 28 2026 (8:02 AM) — day eleven: October 15 becomes the date, remote Flex-Port update comes back, the update dashboard, and "no private chats"

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-28-transactional-mvp1-remote-updates-and-october-15-target.md|eleventh morning call]] (Russian, 8:02–8:45). Eight voices, Val present [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**The date.** October 15 replaces October 1 — Pavel named it, Klimovich called October 1 not achievable, and Val asked for the final state by October 15 "если это возможно". Irina's autotest estimate is October 23. Full record on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-28-october-15-replaces-october-1-as-the-mvp1-date.md]]; the old plan on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-first-transactional-installation-planned-for-october-1.md]] (now superseded) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**Remote Flex-Port update is back in MVP 1** (capability 146), and PORT-13 stays with Alekseev. On [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-remote-firmware-update-is-in-mvp1-and-flex-iq-updates-itself-first.md]] (Sept-28 update), with the terminal-management Updates dashboard Chudinov showed [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**A PIN question for later, not MVP 1.** Chudinov: Flex IQ needs no PIN but FlexPay does, so with two devices at a location a ticket made without a PIN may need to be redeemed on FlexPay. [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]'s idea: with two active gateways, the port always asks for a PIN when a new wallet is created; a player who goes to the cashier gets the ticket found and printed without a PIN. Chudinov proposed fixing it as a rule: always a PIN on the port. Pavel proposed a hidden fixed PIN — "4 единицы, например там всегда 1234" — as the current Flex-Port already does with the old FlexPay; the PIN window is hidden and four ones are entered "под капотом". Chudinov: that breaks once the wallet moves to the cloud, or when Flex IQ stops ignoring the PIN; Pavel: the Flex-Port has the same parameter. Klimovich: "не в [MVP] один очевидно, потому что у нас только [IQ]". Biratka: "это техническая задача… [н]е бизнес". No decision. Asked by Khamin, for MVP 1 Flex IQ cannot create a ticket with a PIN — Chudinov: "Не будет" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**Card errors into the requirements.** Klimovich asked for the new tilts and card errors in the common chat after the call; there is "хороший шар" he updates the requirements, the errors and the text library by the next day's call. Pavel's Wi-Fi QR-code material (with Claude-proposed Wi-Fi error screens) is not MVP 1 and not urgent [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]]. The "no private chats" rule is on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-24-written-records-go-by-email-and-chats-are-for-instant-matters.md]] (Sept-28 update).

**Status (Irina).** All MVP 1 capabilities and requirements are reworked; all autotest features are created; Khamin and Ivan implement them. Ivan is preparing a document, to send the next day, on setting up the machine, port and SAS in Georgia. Detail on [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 28, 8:02 AM) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**Wi-Fi.** Val thought Alekseev focuses on Wi-Fi. Pavel: "вай фай я у него забрал он он только железо сделал вай фай мы сделаем сами… 90% наверное уже сделано." It stays MVP 2 — Irina: "Capolity 145… [флек]спорт вай фай линк". See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-wi-fi-module-on-flex-port-with-an-external-router.md]] (Sept-28 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**The repository standard.** Val asked if work follows the repository standard. Pavel: yes, the Flex-Port platform repository holds the code, prose and contracts; and while Claude updates the prose "он наряду с этим ещё находит баги. И предлагает их поправить" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**Brackets for other makers' machines.** Val, to Klimovich: Flex-Ports need mounting brackets for other makers' machines — "например, если вы хотите установить машину на аристократ". "Я возьму это на себя, но напомните мне, если я упущу это." Klimovich: "я понимаю о чём речь" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**FlexPay updates wait.** Biratka, who works on FlexPay: the server has no update infrastructure for FlexPay yet, which blocks him. Chudinov: a future capability, a FlexPay tab next to Flex IQ, "пока [MVP один] не закроем" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**MVP 2.** Biratka works only on MVP 2 — bill validator, cards, GSM modem — and nobody works on its capabilities. Klimovich: the MVP 2 capabilities and requirements are "совершенно не актуально", and need a business scope first; he returns on the 12th and "что я успею сделать до 12 я не берусь". Biratka will collect business questions — for example a card that is dispensed but not taken: swallow it back into the box? What state is the device in, what errors go to the operator? — and bring them to these calls and by email [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

**Emulators.** Khamin asked who builds emulators. Irina: "Этим занимается Антон." Biratka: Anton made one, but inside FlexPay. Pavel: he must now move it all to Flex IQ. See [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-virtual-test-system-tries-full-arm-emulation-first.md]] (Sept-28 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Remote_Updates_and_October_15_Target-September-28-2026.md]].

## Sept 28 2026 (12:50 PM) — the product set described to investors: ~$4,000 cost, what is easy and hard to make, and the AFT as a peripheral in competitors' machines

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-28-larry-john-eddie-lunch-transactional-equipment-investment-structure.md|Sept-28 lunch]] with [[wiki/people/larry-sharon.md|Larry]] and his partners, and again at the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-28-equipment-depreciation-investor-pitch.md|3:33 PM pitch]] to an unnamed adviser, Val described the transactional family in plain terms for people outside the industry (the investment itself is on [[wiki/projects/transactional-equipment-depreciation-investor-deal.md]]):

- **Three devices.** "We're coming out with a point of sale, which replaces the handheld unit but creates form of automation. We have a self-checkout counter, which is a form of a kiosk. And we have a product which is called, in our industry, called AFT, Automated Fund Transfer." The AFT's wallet: "What we have is temporary electronic wallet… printed in the form of a QR code. It's like a ticket that comes out… Lets you transact between the machines" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Transactional_Equipment_Investment_Structure-September-28-2026.md]].
- **Cost and price.** "The combination of all these devices, my cost is roughly four thousand dollars. We retail it at approximately fifteen thousand dollars" (Larry: "sixteen thousand dollars, which is what your retail report shows") [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Transactional_Equipment_Investment_Structure-September-28-2026.md]]; "retail price is sixteen thousand dollars" and "a spread of one to four" to the adviser [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Equipment_Depreciation_Investor_Pitch-September-28-2026.md]].
- **What is easy and hard to build.** "The point of sale is easy because it's all mold injection. It's plastic and assembles very quickly. The AFT displays are easy because it's molded… But the self-serve kiosk is probably the hardest, but the most desirable" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Transactional_Equipment_Investment_Structure-September-28-2026.md]].
- **Into competitors' machines.** "The AFTs are installed inside of the competitors' machines… it's considered to be a peripheral device, and it's open architecture, and it connects to an open standard port, which is tested by an independent laboratory." Permission: "Your own permission. It belongs to master distributor" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Transactional_Equipment_Investment_Structure-September-28-2026.md]].
- **Service.** Advance replacement on everything, "approximately two percent replacement cost" — "if it's a self serving kiosk, it will be twenty machines per thousand" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Transactional_Equipment_Investment_Structure-September-28-2026.md]]. To the adviser: "It's a solid-state electronics. There's no mechanical part. It's an optical sensor. It's a touch screen"; on redemption fraud, "Zero chance" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Equipment_Depreciation_Investor_Pitch-September-28-2026.md]].
- **No creativity needed.** "There's no creativity in transactional equipment. It's very very binary… You don't need to razzle dazzle your people" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Larry_Transactional_Equipment_Investment_Structure-September-28-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** none.

## Sept 29 2026 (8:06 AM) — day twelve: the tilts document is still missing, Ivan's set-up instruction is walked through, three documents go to Confluence for Elizabeth, "always the latest build", and MikroTik is the router

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-29-transactional-mvp1-setup-instructions-and-mikrotik-router.md|twelfth morning call]] (Russian, 8:03–8:31 in two segments; the first three minutes are waiting and small talk). Nine voices, Val present [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].

**The tilts document.** [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]] had no news of his own and had not received the collection of new tilts and errors he asked for the day before; he asked for it by email. [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]]: "в процессе я заканчиваю эту задачу и будет" — so the Sept-28 requirements update (above) is blocked on it. On [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-24-written-records-go-by-email-and-chats-are-for-instant-matters.md]] (Sept-29, 8:06 AM update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].

**Ivan's set-up instruction**, sent that morning, walked through on screen: the Flex-Port and Flex IQ already set up, the cables PC–machine and Flex-Port–machine, the SAS set-up on the machine, the SAS emulator, and the Eclipse long-poll commands with what the machine should show. **The SAS checkbox on the machine must be on the port where the Flex-Port is connected, or the Flex-Port does not work.** [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]] on the Flex-Port's SAS icon: red = nothing connected, yellow = SAS commands flowing, light blue (he first said green) = registered. Still missing: how to flash the Flex-Port ([[wiki/people/alexei-khamin.md|Khamin]] updates his old instruction) and how to install Flex IQ (none exists). All three go into Confluence. Then [[wiki/people/elizabeth.md|Elizabeth]] builds the full set-up in Georgia; when it works, other makers' machines come to the warehouse. The SAS tool zip went to Elizabeth by email and should sit where every JVL employee can reach it. Full record on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-negative-cases-first-and-flex-ports-tested-in-borrowed-non-jvl-machines.md]] (Sept-29 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].

Elizabeth can see the Updates tab (checked with "Сергей", not resolved). On [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-remote-firmware-update-is-in-mvp1-and-flex-iq-updates-itself-first.md]] (Sept-29, 8:06 AM update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].
