Pavel will ask Alekseev whether the firmware is ready [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].

**Autotests.** [[wiki/people/vladimir-samusenko.md|Vladimir]] started that day; Khamin and Ivan continue; [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]] sets up her own environment. Pavel: the test tasks in the schedule show end dates of yesterday but are still in work; Irina: "Правильно". On [[wiki/topics/test-automation-strategy.md]] (Sept 29, 8:06 AM) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].

**MVP 2.** Klimovich: "Александр по MVP 2… Что надо обсудить?" [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]]: he will write emails as agreed; "пока у меня такого именно срочного ничего нет" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].

**The router.** [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]]'s report — Linksys does not come up without internet, Netgear cannot be tested in Belarus, MikroTik can be bought in Minsk at $40–$100 — met Val's "Мы не можем пользоваться китайским… оборудованием"; Val left the brand to the team and Klimovich closed on MikroTik. A switch is needed as well (routers have three or four ports); the same switch model should be chosen in Minsk because Chudinov once had devices that did not see each other through one. Pavel's PoE idea — power the ports from the switch so they stay online when the machine is off — needs Flex-Port hardware that does not exist today; future only. Full record on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-29-mikrotik-is-the-location-router-and-val-leaves-the-brand-to-the-team.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** none.

### Open questions (Sept 29 2026, 8:06 AM)

- Should a future Flex-Port revision take power over Ethernet from a PoE switch, so the ports stay online when the machine is off? Pavel raised it for future capabilities only; Bosik: the hardware would have to support it — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Setup_Instructions_and_MikroTik_Router-September-29-2026.md]]

## Sept 30 2026 (4:57 AM stand-up) — FlexPay's modem capability 247 connects on a SIM, the LED service instead of the HAL, Chudinov's 152 / 175 / 241, and Tsymanov's 158

At the [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-30-daily-standup-flexport-firmware-delivery-and-network-loss.md|Sept-30 Minsk stand-up]] (Russian) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_FlexPort_Firmware_Delivery_and_Network_Loss-September-30-2026.md]]:

- **[[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]] — capability 247, the modem for FlexPay.** "я вчера создал новую [capability], связанную с [модемом] для flexpay. Её не было это [capability] 247" — he created it the day before, with features under it; "большинство фич уже завершились, только остались проверки". He took a SIM card from Pavel that morning — "взял… Симкарту и сейчас начал проверки, но она… всё уже подключилась, то есть сеть есть" — and expects to close it that day. This is the "GS[M]" task that was "not started" on Sept 29, now under way (evolution).
- **The LED strip and the LED service.** On the LED strip Pavel had given him: "В библиотеке [HAL] там есть. Уже реализация ленты" — the HAL library already implements the strip — "[н]о… [API] не вынесены для неё". FlexPay "работает на хале" today. The alternative is a Linux-daemon service "по аналогии с [билвалидатором], по аналогии с принтером". Pavel: it "существует сейчас в 2 экземплярах, один, который я написал когдато" — a test of the new-firmware approach, lying "в проекте… [г]де прошивки… для всех плат" — "а 2 это который написал… Антон" (not resolved, §4), who "сразу ещё писал сверху оболочку джаваскую" so it can be used as a Java library; "женя там уже давал замечание Антону, он уже фиксел это" and [[wiki/people/evgeny-kharetski.md|Kharetski]] is already moving the data-centre side onto that service ("женя сейчас это делает"). Biratka: "я попробую наверно уже с сервисом работать уже [с HAL] не буду бороться" — his next work is LEDs and the switch to the service. The derived notes call this an agreement between him and Pavel [[raw/Meeting_Notes_Daily_Standup_FlexPort_Firmware_Delivery_and_Network_Loss-September-30-2026.md]].
- **[[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Chudinov]].** "Завершается кэп 152 это всё по активации, когда там нашлись коекакие проблемы"; "в работе кэп 175" (what it is: "рекорд. Пресса" — **unclear**), which "еще будет дорабатываться". Pavel asked him to set a start date on FIQU-86 ("я поставил 28" — Pavel had set the 28th). **Capability 241** — "то, что Андрей создавал, ни на кого не [засайнил]" (Klimovich by context) — now has his features: "информации о подключенной машине… машина подключилась, отключилась и статус там… [out of] сервис со списком причин… плюс там же [логи] по сассу, которые мы будем… потом передавать… Кроме пингов". He can write the protocol on the Flex IQ side first — "ты потом будешь под него подстраиваться" — and start that day; Pavel: "Хорошо." He will raise the sounds topic at the later meeting — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-30-flex-iq-sound-feedback-and-the-flexpay-slim-led-language-go-to-mvp-2.md]].
- **[[wiki/people/sergey-tsymanov.md|Tsymanov]].** "Закончил задачу созданием [волета] через [Флекс-Порт]" (capability 156 by the Sept-29 report — **inference**; he gives no number). On 158: "там есть куча задач ну 4 задачи"; some to drop — "допустим [pay mament hop 5]" (garbled) — "потому что у нас [волеты] будут храниться пока. На терминале"; much is already built; "планирую закрыть сегодня следующую взять".
- **Firmware delivery, network loss, the emulator.** On [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-14-remote-firmware-update-is-in-mvp1-and-flex-iq-updates-itself-first.md]] (Sept-30 update), [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-24-a-flex-port-that-dies-holding-money-sends-it-to-unclaimed-and-one-without-a-gateway-goes-out-of-service.md]] (Sept-30 update) and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-virtual-test-system-tries-full-arm-emulation-first.md]] (Sept-30 update).

**§15.8:** clear. **§15 #9:** none.

### Open commitments (Sept 30 2026, 4:57 AM)

- [ ] [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Alexander Biratka]] — finish the checks and close capability 247 (the modem for FlexPay) — due Sept 30 2026 ("сегодня, возможно") — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_FlexPort_Firmware_Delivery_and_Network_Loss-September-30-2026.md]]
- [ ] [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Alexander Biratka]] — next, the LEDs on FlexPay, working through the LED service (Anton's copy, with the Java wrapper) rather than the HAL — due unspecified — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_FlexPort_Firmware_Delivery_and_Network_Loss-September-30-2026.md]]
- [ ] [[wiki/people/dmitri-chudinov.md|Dmitri Chudinov]] — start the capability 241 protocol on the Flex IQ side (machine connected / disconnected, out-of-service with reasons, SAS logs without pings); Pavel adapts the port side to it — due started Sept 30 2026 — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_FlexPort_Firmware_Delivery_and_Network_Loss-September-30-2026.md]]
- [ ] [[wiki/people/sergey-tsymanov.md|Sergey Tsymanov]] — close one capability-158 (zero balance) task that day and take the next; drop the ones that no longer apply while wallets stay on the terminal — due Sept 30 2026 — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Daily_Standup_FlexPort_Firmware_Delivery_and_Network_Loss-September-30-2026.md]]

## Sept 30 2026 (8:03 AM) — day thirteen: the three instructions are in Confluence, test devices and the four boards, the recycler ships, MVP 2 is FlexPay only plus Wi-Fi, a spare-parts email, the modem supply, and Flex IQ sound to MVP 2

The [[wiki/meetings/2026-09-30-transactional-mvp1-instructions-complete-and-modem-supply.md|thirteenth morning call]] (Russian, 8:03–8:24). Eight voices, Val absent [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].

**The instructions.** All three are in or due in Confluence; Elizabeth starts the Georgia set-up tomorrow with a shared chat behind her — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-negative-cases-first-and-flex-ports-tested-in-borrowed-non-jvl-machines.md]] (Sept-30 update), [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-30-elizabeths-georgia-set-up-questions-go-to-a-shared-teams-chat-not-one-person.md]], [[wiki/topics/docs-as-code-and-atlassian-exit.md]] (Sept 30) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].

**Test devices.** [[wiki/people/irina-shishko.md|Irina]]: the testers "айкью делим… остальные ждут" — one Flex IQ is shared. [[wiki/people/pavel.md|Pavel]]: "можно ещё один флекс [IQ]… не в виде готового устройства, а в виде… плат лежащих на столе… для тестов этого достаточно." [[wiki/people/alexander-biratka.md|Biratka]]: "Можно еще один распечатать?" — Pavel: half the parts are not needed; Irina: only one side; "крышки не надо". For FlexPay "с корпусами у нас вообще нету" (Biratka); "может быть гена может там чтото похожее дать?" Pavel: "из пластмассы мы такое не сделаем, там большие детали… экран больше, чем печатное поле твоего принтера"; Biratka and [[wiki/people/andrey-bosik.md|Bosik]]: parts can be printed with tabs and holes and glued "прям как Монолит". But "панели нет той, которая нам надо" — Pavel: "у нас телевизор один только с правильным разрешением" — Biratka: "Тогда смысла нету." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]]

**The boards.** Pavel: "у нас всего 4. Из них… 2 флекса айкью и один flex pay и одна плата… не используется". Biratka: "моя плата… со сгоревшим [USB] одной" — one burned USB port. Pavel: "к нам должны приехать ещё в ближайшее время пару плат. То есть в принципе это нам закроет весь парк наших устройств для тестирования." [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]]

**The recycler.** The chosen bill recycler is on its way to Minsk, "через пару недель"; it belongs to the Flex Recycler project, "3 будет" — on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-10-requirements-come-first-and-the-recycler-is-out-of-both-mvps.md]] (Sept-30 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].

**MVP 2.** [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Klimovich]]: when he returns, MVP 2 centres on FlexPay in the slim form factor with all screens and flow current. Pavel: "[MVP] 2 это исключительно добавление [FlexPay]… все остальное это уже [MVP] 3" — two devices at one location "наверное 3"; Wi-Fi connect stays MVP 2 — on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-09-one-transactional-unit-per-location-until-the-wi-fi-iteration.md]] (Sept-30 update) and [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-07-wi-fi-module-on-flex-port-with-an-external-router.md]] (Sept-30 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].

**Spare parts.** Klimovich: "может быть инициировать письмо на Геннадия [и] вала и сказать, что давайте актуализируем. Набор запчастей в Минске, чтобы у вас всё было в доступе… я просто запущу этот процесс обсуждения"; the team then tells [[wiki/people/genadi-peari.md|Genadi]] what it has and he says what to send. Pavel: "мы покупали этот мониторчик. Нужного разрешения нужна диагонали" — a second FlexPay test device would need another such monitor; Biratka: "может закажем оригинальную панель хоть одну?" — Pavel: "Ну давай хорошо"; Klimovich: discuss it in the email; he adds Pavel, and Pavel asks for Irina too, "чтобы она была в курсе… что у нас болит и что мы хотим вылечить" [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].

**Modems.** Which model, one or two per device, nobody knows; Genadi is with the suppliers; 100 asked for, 5 or 10 offered, 1,000 minimums; the current models are end of life; about 30 more of the same model are being bought on Val's order — on [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-04-north-america-modem-variant-and-ask-kore-for-the-apn.md]] (Sept-30 update) [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].

**Flex IQ sound.** A capability nobody had listed; speakers are inside; it goes to MVP 2 with the FlexPay slim LED language — [[wiki/decisions/2026-09-30-flex-iq-sound-feedback-and-the-flexpay-slim-led-language-go-to-mvp-2.md]] [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]].

**§15.8:** clear. Procurement quantities and supplier minimums are company facts. **§15 #9:** none.

### Open commitments (Sept 30 2026, 8:03 AM)

- [ ] [[wiki/people/andrei-klimovich.md|Andrei Klimovich]] — start the email to Genadi Peari and Val to bring the Minsk spare-parts set up to date (including the second monitor or an original panel for a second FlexPay test device), with Pavel and Irina copied — due unspecified — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]]

### Open questions (Sept 30 2026, 8:03 AM)

- A second monitor like the one bought, or at least one original panel, for a second FlexPay test device? Moved to the spare-parts email — [[raw/Meeting_Transcript_Transactional_MVP1_Instructions_Complete_and_Modem_Supply-September-30-2026.md]]
